# Rashi on Nehemiah

רש"י על נחמיה

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sefaria vocalized edition (Segments: 451, License: unknown)

Version (En): The Judaica Press complete Tanach with Rashi, translated by A. J. Rosenberg (Segments: 450)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:1:1)


**דִּבְרֵי נְחֶמְיָה וְגוֹ׳.** מִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ כָּתַב נְחֶמְיָה סֵפֶר זֶה:


**The words of Nehemiah, etc.** Nehemiah wrote this book from here on.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:1:2)


**שְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים.** הִיא **שְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים** לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהוּא אַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתָּא. וּבְמַסֶּכֶת רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה מָצָאנוּ: **שְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים** ״שְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים״ לִגְזֵרָה שָׁוָה, מִן הַכָּתוּב שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְמַטָּה ״וַיְהִי בְחֹדֶשׁ נִיסָן שְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים לְאַרְתַּחְשַׁשְׂתָּא וְגוֹ׳״ (נחמיה ב:א). וּמֵחֹדֶשׁ תִּשְׁרֵי הָיוּ מוֹנִין לְחֶשְׁבּוֹן הַשָּׁנִים, וְעַל כֵּן נֶאֱמַר כָּאן: **וַיְהִי בְחֹדֶשׁ כִּסְלֵו שְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים וְגוֹ׳**, וְהוּא **כִּסְלֵו** הַבָּא לְאַחַר תִּשְׁרֵי, וּלְמַטָּה נֶאֱמַר ״וַיְהִי בְחֹדֶשׁ נִיסָן שְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים וְגוֹמֵר״, הוּא נִיסָן שֶׁבְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה. וְאֵינִי יָכוֹל לִפְתֹּר וְלוֹמַר מִנִּיסָן הָיוּ מוֹנִין לְחֶשְׁבּוֹן שְׁנוֹתֵיהֶם, וְאוֹתוֹ מַעֲשֶׂה הַכָּתוּב לְמַטָּה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיְהִי בְחֹדֶשׁ נִיסָן וְגוֹ׳״ הָיָה קֹדֶם מַעֲשֶׂה זֶה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר **וַיְהִי בְחֹדֶשׁ כִּסְלֵו שְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים וְגוֹ׳**, וְאֵין מֻקְדָּם וּמְאֻחָר בָּהֶם, דָּבָר זֶה אֵינִי יָכוֹל לִפְתֹּר וְלוֹמַר, וְהַפָּרָשִׁיּוֹת מוֹכִיחוֹת, שֶׁאַף אוֹתוֹ מַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁל פָּרָשָׁה שְׁנִיָּה לֹא הָיְתָה כִּי אִם בִּשְׁבִיל מַעֲשֶׂה זֶה שֶׁל פָּרָשָׁה זֹאת רִאשׁוֹנָה:


**the twentieth year** This refers to the twentieth year of King Darius, who is identical with Artaxerxes. In Tractate Rosh Hashanah (3a, b), we find that the phrase “the twentieth year” is mentioned twice using similar wording [to teach us that it is speaking of the identical year]. It states below (2:1), “And it came to pass in the month of Nissan of the twentieth year of Artaxerxes, etc.” The years were calculated from the month of Tishrei. It says here, “And it was in the month of Kislev of the twentieth year, etc.” This refers to the Kislev coming after Tishrei. Further down it says (2:1), “And it was in the month of Nissan of the twentieth year, etc.” This refers to the Nissan of the same year. And I cannot explain that they calculated their years from the month of Nissan and that the incident mentioned below: “And it was in the month of Nissan, etc.” occurred before this incident: “And it was in the month of Kislev, etc.” It is true that events [in the Torah] are not necessarily recorded in chronological order. Nevertheless, the chapters indicate that the second chapter took place only because of the events of the first chapter.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:2:1)


**אֶחָד מֵאַחַי**. אֶחָד מֵחֲבֵרַי:


**one of my brethren** one of my companions.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:2:2)


**וָאֶשְׁאָלֵם עַל הַיְּהוּדִים וְגוֹ׳.** שָׁאַלְתִּי לָהֶם עַל עִנְיַן פְּלֵיטַת הַיְּהוּדִים אֲשֶׁר נִשְׁאֲרוּ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם מִן הַשֶּׁבִי שֶׁל בָּבֶל:


**and I asked them about the Jews** I asked them about the Jews who had escaped the Babylonian captivity and remained in Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:2:3)


**הַפְּלֵיטָה.** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁנִּצּוֹלוּ מִגָּלוּת בָּבֶל:


**who had escaped** because they were saved from the Babylonian exile.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:2:4)


**וְעַל יְרוּשָׁלִָם.** וְעַל עִנְיַן הָעִיר שָׁאַלְתִּי אֲלֵיהֶם:


**and about Jerusalem** And I asked them about the city.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:3:1)


**וַיֹּאמְרוּ לִי.** הֵשִׁיבוּ לִי עַל רִאשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן וְעַל אַחֲרוֹן אַחֲרוֹן, תְּחִלָּה עַל עִנְיַן הַיְּהוּדִים וְאַחַר כָּךְ עַל עִנְיַן הַחוֹמוֹת וְהַשְּׁעָרִים:


**And they said to me** They answered my first question first and my latter question last; first, concerning the Jews and afterwards, concerning the wall and the gates.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:3:2)


**הַנִּשְׁאָרִים וְגוֹ׳.** אוֹתָם הַיְּהוּדִים אֲשֶׁר נִשְׁאֲרוּ שָׁם בִּמְדִינַת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, הֵם נְתוּנִים **בְּרָעָה גְדוֹלָה וּבְחֶרְפָּה**, לְפִי שֶׁהָעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים בּוֹזְזִים וְשׁוֹלְלִים אוֹתָם:


**who remained** Those Jews who remained in the province of Jerusalem are in great trouble and reproach because the gentiles plunder and pillage them.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:3:3)


**וְחוֹמַת יְרוּשָׁלִַם מְפֹרָצֶת.** וְחוֹמַת הָעִיר הֲרֵי הִיא פְּרוּצָה, וְהַשְּׁעָרִים שְׂרוּפִים **בָּאֵשׁ** כְּקַדְמָתָן; שֶׁיִּשְׂרָאֵל לֹא בָּנוּ חוֹמוֹת הָעִיר וּשְׁעָרֶיהָ כִּי אִם בִּנְיַן בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ לְבַדּוֹ:


**and the wall of Jerusalem is breached** And the wall of the city is breached, and the gates are burned with fire as they were before, for Israel had not built the wall of the city or its gates, only the Temple.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:3:4)


**נִצְּתוּ.** כְּמוֹ נוּצְתוּ (ספרים אחרים: נִצְּתוּ כמו נִתְּצוּ):


**were burned** Heb. נִצְתוּ, like נֻצְתוּ.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:4:1)


**יָמִים**. כַּמָּה יָמִים:


**for days** Heb. יָמִים, many days.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:5:1)


**אָנָּא**. לְשׁוֹן בַּקָּשָׁה וּתְחִנָּה:


**I beseech You** Heb. אָנָא, an expression of request and supplication.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:6:1)


**קַשֶּׁבֶת.** כְּמוֹ קוֹשֶׁבֶת כּוּ׳, כַּאֲשֶׁר יֹאמַר מֵ״אָהַב״ (בראשית כז:ט) ״אוֹהֵב״ (שמואל א יח:טז), כֵּן יֹאמַר מִן קָשַׁב קוֹשֵׁב וְקוֹשֶׁבֶת:


**attentive** Heb. קַשֶּׁבֶת, like קוֹשֶּׁבֶת, listening, for just as one says from אוֹהֵב, אָהַב, so does one say from קָשַּׁב, קוֹשֵּׁב and קוֹשֶּׁבֶת.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:7:1)


**חֲבוֹל**. שֵׁם דָּבָר, לְשׁוֹן הַשְׁחָתָה. וְחֵי״ת נִנְקְדָה בַּחֲטַף פַּתָּח. וְאֵינִי יָכוֹל לְפוֹתְרוֹ בִּלְשׁוֹן פָּעוּל, שֶׁאִם כֵּן הָיָה לוֹ לִנְקֹד חֵי״ת בְּקָמַץ גָּדוֹל:


**We have dealt corruptly** Heb. חֲבֹל, a noun, an expression of corruption, since the “heth” is vowelized with a “hataf pattah,” and I cannot interpret it as a verb form, because in that case, it would have to be vowelized with a large “kamatz.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:7:2)


**חֲבַלְנוּ.** לְשׁוֹן הַשְׁחָתָה, כְּמוֹ (דניאל ו:כג) ״חֲבוּלָא לָא עֲבַדְתְּ״, וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**we have dealt corruptly** Heb. חָבַלְנוּ, an expression of corruption, as in Daniel (6:23): “I have done no harm.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:9:1)


**אִם יִהְיֶה נִדַּחֲכֶם**. כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״אִם יִהְיֶה נִדַּחֲךָ וְגוֹ׳״ (דְּבָרִים ל:ד):


**if your exile is** like the matter that is stated (Deut. 30:4): “If your exile is at the end of the heavens, etc.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:11:1)


**אָנָּא אֲדֹנָי תְּהִי נָא אָזְנְךָ קַשֶּׁבֶת.** בִּתְחִלַּת תְּפִלָּתוֹ אָמַר לָשׁוֹן זֶה שֶׁל תְּחִנָּה, וְכֵן לְאַחַר תְּפִלָּתוֹ:


**I beseech You, O Lord, may Your ear now be attentive** At the beginning of his prayer he used this expression, and so after his prayer.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:11:2)


**לְעַבְדְּךָ הַיּוֹם וְגוֹ׳.** כָּךְ הָיָה מִתְפַּלֵּל עַל עַצְמוֹ:


**Your servant** This is what he prayed for himself.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:11:3)


**לִפְנֵי הָאִישׁ הַזֶּה.** לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**before this man** before the king.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 1:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/1:11:4)


**מַשְׁקֶה.** כְּמוֹ (בראשית מ:א) ״מַשְׁקֵה לְמֶלֶךְ מִצְרָיִם״, וְזֶה שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי לְמַעְלָה (עזרא ב:סג). כְּדִבְרֵי רַבּוֹתֵינוּ: הַתִּרְשָׁתָא הוּא נְחֶמְיָה:


**butler** Heb. מַשְּׁקֶה, like (Gen. 40:1): “...the butler of (מַשְּׁקֶה) the king of Egypt.” This is what I explained above (Ezra 2:63) according to the words of our Sages, that Hattirshatha is Nehemiah.



## Chapter 2


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:1:1)


**לְאַרְתַּחְשַׁסְתְּא**. הוּא דָּרְיָוֶשׁ (עזרא ד:ה), וְעַל שֵׁם הַמַּלְכוּת נִקְרָא כָּךְ:


**of...Artaxerxes** That was Darius, and because of his kingship he was called this.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:1:2)


**יַיִן לְפָנָיו.** הֵבִיאוּ יַיִן לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ בֶּחָצֵר:


**wine before him** They brought wine before the king in the court.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:1:3)


**וָאֶשָּׂא אֶת הַיַּיִן.** וְאָנֹכִי נָשָׂאתִי אֶת כּוֹס הַיַּיִן וּנְתַתִּיהָ לַמֶּלֶךְ. וְכָךְ הוּא הַמִּנְהָג, שֶׁהַמֵּבִיא יַיִן בַּחֲצַר הַמֶּלֶךְ אֵינוֹ נוֹשֵׂא וְנוֹתְנוֹ לַמֶּלֶךְ, אֲבָל שַׂר הַמַּשְׁקִים מְקַבֵּל כּוֹס הַיַּיִן מִיַּד הַמֵּבִיא וְהוּא נוֹשֵׂא וְנוֹתְנוֹ לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**and I carried the wine** And I carried the cup of wine and gave it to the king, and so is the custom, that the one who brings wine into the king’s court does not carry it and give it to the king, but the chief butler receives it from the hand of the one who brings it, and carries it and gives it to the king.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:1:4)


**לֹא הָיִיתִי רַע לְפָנָיו.** וַאֲנִי לֹא הָיִיתִי רָגִיל לִהְיוֹת רַע לְפָנָיו בְּרוֹעַ פָּנִים, כִּי אִם שָׂמֵחַ וְטוֹב לֵב, וְעַתָּה הָיִיתִי זוֹעֵף בְּרוֹעַ פָּנִים:


**And I had never been sad in his presence** And I was not accustomed to being sad in his presence, to have a sad face, but to be happy and cheerful, but now I was sad with a sad face.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:2:1)


**וְאַתָּה אֵינְךָ חוֹלֶה.** וַהֲרֵי אֵין אַתָּה חוֹלֶה:


**You are not ill** Is it not so that you are not ill?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:2:2)


**אֵין זֶה.** אֵין הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הַזֶּה **כִּי אִם רֹעַ לֵב**, שֶׁיֵּשׁ בִּלְבָבְךָ עָלַי לַהֲמִיתֵנִי בְּסַם הַמָּוֶת בִּשְׁתִיַּת כּוֹס זֶה:


**This is nothing** This incident is only wickedness of heart, that you have in your heart to put me to death by poisoning this cup.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:2:3)


**וָאִירָא הַרְבֵּה מְאֹד.** וְנִתְיָרֵאתִי מְאֹד עַל כָּךְ, שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲלִיל עָלַי הַמֶּלֶךְ עֲלִילָה:


**And I became very frightened** And I became very frightened about this, lest the king accuse me falsely.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:3:1)


**מַדּוּעַ לֹא יֵרְעוּ פָנָי.** מַדּוּעַ לֹא יִהְיוּ פָנַי רָעִים:


**Why should my countenance not be sad** [as translated,] why should my countenance not be sad.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:3:2)


**אֲשֶׁר הָעִיר** יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, שֶׁהִיא בֵּית קִבְרוֹת אֲבוֹתַי, חֲרֵבָה:


**when the city** Jerusalem, which is the place of the graves of my forefathers, is laid waste.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:4:1)


**עַל מַה זֶּה אַתָּה מְבַקֵּשׁ.** אֵי זֶה דָּבָר תְּבַקֵּשׁ מִמֶּנִּי וְאֶעֱשֶׂה לְךָ:


**For what do you make request** What do you request of me and I shall do it for you.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:4:2)


**וָאֶתְפַּלֵּל.** וְאָמַרְתִּי לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ בִּתְפִלָּתִי: כֵּן יְהִי רָצוֹן מִלְּפָנֵי **אֱלֹהֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם**, שֶׁתְּמַלֵּא שְׁאֵלָתִי וּבַקָּשָׁתִי:


**And I prayed** And I said before the king in my prayer, “May it be Your will, O God of the heavens, that You fulfill my petition and my request.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:5:1)


**אֲשֶׁר תִּשְׁלָחֵנִי.** שֶׁתִּתֵּן לִי רְשׁוּת לַעֲלוֹת לִירוּשָׁלַיִם וְלִבְנוֹתָהּ:


**that you send me** that you grant me permission to ascend to Jerusalem and build it.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר לִי הַמֶּלֶךְ.** בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁדִּבֵּר אֵלַי הַמֶּלֶךְ הָיְתָה אִשְׁתּוֹ הַמַּלְכָּה יוֹשֶׁבֶת אֶצְלוֹ:


**And the king said to me** At the time the king spoke to me, his wife, the queen, was sitting beside him.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:2)


**וְהַשֵּׁגַל.** אִשְׁתּוֹ הַמַּלְכָּה, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דניאל ה:ב) ״שֵׁגְלָתֵהּ וּלְחֵנָתֵהּ״. שֶׁכֵּן נוֹהֵג הַמִּקְרָא לִכְתֹּב הַמַּלְכָּה אֵצֶל הַפִּלֶגֶשׁ ״נָשָׁיו וּפִילַגְשָׁיו״ (דברי הימים ב יא:כא), כְּמוֹ ״מְלָכוֹת וּפִילַגְשִׁים״ (שיר השירים ו:ט):


**and the queen** Heb. וְהַשֵּׁגָל, his wife the queen, as it is stated (Dan. 5:2): “...his queen (שֵּׁגְלָתֵהּ) and his concubine,” for it is customary for Scripture to write “the queen,” next to “the concubine”; his wives and his concubines, like (Song 6:9): “מְלָכוֹת וּפִּילַגְשִּׁים, queens and concubines. “


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:3)


**עַד מָתַי יִהְיֶה מַהֲלָכְךָ.** מָתַי תִּרְצֶה שֶׁיְּהֵא מַהֲלָכְךָ וּמַסָּעֲךָ?:


**How long will your trip take** When is it your intention to return?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:4)


**וּמָתַי תָּשׁוּב.** וּמָתַי דַּעְתְּךָ לַחֲזוֹר?:


**and when will you return?** When is it your intention to return?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:5)


**יִשְׁלָחֵנִי.** נָתַן לִי רְשׁוּת לָלֶכֶת:


**and he sent me** He gave me permission to go.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:6:6)


**וָאֶתְּנָה לוֹ זְמָן.** נָתַתִּי לוֹ זְמָן לַחֲזוֹר:


**and I gave him a time** I gave him a time of return.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:7:1)


**אִגְּרוֹת יִתְּנוּ לִי.** סוֹפְרֶיךָ עַל רְשׁוּתְךָ וְדַעְתְּךָ, חֲתוּמוֹת בְּחוֹתָמְךָ, עַל פַּחֲוַת עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר שֶׁל צַד אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**may letters be given to me** [Let] your scribes [give me letters] by your sanction and your knowledge, sealed with your signet to the governors of the land beyond the river, on the side of the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:7:2)


**אֲשֶׁר יַעֲבִירוּנִי.** וִיתַיְּרוּנִי לְשָׁלוֹם:


**that they escort me through** and escort me in peace.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:7:3)


**עַד אֲשֶׁר אָבוֹא.** לִירוּשָׁלַיִם אֲשֶׁר בִּמְדִינַת יְהוּדָה:


**until I come** to Jerusalem, which is in the province of Judea.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:1)


**וְאִגֶּרֶת אֶל אָסָף.** וְתִשְׁלַח כְּתַב אִגֶּרֶת אֶל אָסָף:


**And a letter to Asaph** and that you send a letter to Asaph.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:2)


**שׁוֹמֵר הַפַּרְדֵּס.** מְמֻנֶּה הָיָה עַל הַיְּעָרִים אֲשֶׁר לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**the guardian of the king’s orchard** He was appointed over the king’s forests.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:3)


**לְקָרוֹת.** מִשְׁקַל חָזָק לַעֲשׂוֹת תִּקְרוֹת וְקוֹרוֹת לְשַׁעֲרֵי בִּירוֹת הַר הַבַּיִת, שֶׁהֲרֵי בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ בָּנוּ בְּבִנְיָנוֹ, אֲבָל שַׁעֲרֵי חוֹמוֹת הַר הַבַּיִת וְחוֹמַת הֶחָצֵר אֲשֶׁר סָבִיב בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ לֹא בָּנוּ עֲדַיִן:


**to make beams** This is the intensive conjugation: to make ceilings and beams for the gates of the castles of the Temple Mount, for they had built the Temple as it should be built, but the gates of the walls of the Temple Mount and the wall for the court surrounding the Temple had not yet been built.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:4)


**וּלְחוֹמַת הָעִיר.** וְלַעֲשׂוֹת קוֹרוֹת **לְחוֹמַת הָעִיר**:


**and for the wall of the city** and to make beams for the wall of the city.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:5)


**וְלַבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר אָבוֹא אֵלָיו.** וְלַעֲשׂוֹת בַּיִת אֲשֶׁר אֵשֵׁב בּוֹ לְצָרְכִּי:


**and for the house to which I shall go** and to make a house in which I shall dwell for my needs.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:6)


**וַיִּתֶּן לִי הַמֶּלֶךְ.** אֶת כָּל אֲשֶׁר שָׁאַלְתִּי מִמֶּנּוּ:


**and the king gave me** all I asked him for.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:8:7)


**כְּיַד.** כְּפִי יָדוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהָיְתָה **טוֹבָה עָלַי** לְהַצְלִיחֵנִי:


**according to the good hand** according to the hand of the Holy One, blessed be He, which was good upon me, to cause me to succeed.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:9:1)


**וָאֶתְּנָה לָהֶם אֶת אִגְּרוֹת הַמֶּלֶךְ.** לְמַעַן יְתַיְּרוּנִי לְשָׁלוֹם:


**and I gave them the king’s letters** in order that they escort me in peace.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:9:2)


**שָׂרֵי חַיִל וּפָרָשִׁים.** שָׁלַח הַמֶּלֶךְ עִמִּי לִכְבוֹדִי, לְמַעַן אֵלֵךְ לְשָׁלוֹם:


**officers of the army and horsemen** the king sent with me [officers] in my honor, in order that I go in peace.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:10:1)


**וַיִּשְׁמַע סַנְבַלַּט וְגוֹ׳.** כַּאֲשֶׁר שָׁמְעוּ שֶׁבָּאתִי לְבַקֵּשׁ טוֹבָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלִבְנוֹת חוֹמוֹת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, וַיֵּרַע בְּעֵינֵיהֶם, כִּי הָיוּ צָרֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין (עזרא ד:א):


**Now Sanballat etc., heard** When they heard that I had come to request good for Israel and to build the walls of Jerusalem it displeased them greatly, for they were the adversaries of Judah and Benjamin.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:11:1)


**וָאֱהִי שָׁם יָמִים שְׁלֹשָׁה.** הָיִיתִי בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם יָמִים שְׁלֹשָׁה וּלְסוֹף שְׁלֹשֶׁת יָמִים קַמְתִּי בַלַּיְלָה אֲנִי וַאֲנָשִׁים מְעַטִּים עִמִּי וְלֹא הִגַּדְתִּי לְשׁוּם אָדָם אֶת אֲשֶׁר נָתַן הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּלִבִּי:


**and I was there for three days** I was in Jerusalem for three days, and at the end of three days, I and a few men with me arose at night, and I did not tell any person what the Holy One, blessed be He, had put into my heart.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:12:1)


**וּבְהֵמָה אֵין עִמִּי.** כִּי כָל הָאֲנָשִׁים מוּעָטִים אֲשֶׁר עִמִּי הָלְכוּ בְרַגְלֵיהֶם, אֲבָל אֲנִי לְבַדִּי הָיִיתִי רוֹכֵב עַל בְּהֵמָה; שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ רוֹצִים לִרְכֹּב עַל סוּסֵיהֶם, לְמַעַן יִהְיוּ יוֹצְאִים מִן הָעִיר בְּצִנְעָא, בְּלֹא יְדִיעַת אָדָם. וְהֵם הָלְכוּ לִשְׁבֹּר וּלְהַפִּיל חוֹמוֹת הָעִיר בַּלַּיְלָה, וּלְהַרְבּוֹת פִּרְצוֹתֶיהָ, לְמַעַן יִהְיוּ בְּנֵי הָעִיר לְמָחָר לְהוּטִים וּמַסְכִּימִים בְּעֵצָה אַחַת עִם נְחֶמְיָה לִבְנוֹת חוֹמוֹת הָעִיר, וְכֵן מוֹכִיחַ הַמַּעֲשֶׂה בְּתוֹךְ הַפָּרָשָׁה:


**and there were no animals with me** The few men who were with me went by foot and I alone rode on an animal. They did not ride their horses because they wanted to leave the city in secret, without anyone knowing. They went to break and cast down the walls of the city at night and to increase their breaches in order that on the morrow, the people of the city would be eager and agree unanimously with Nehemiah to build the walls of the city. So it appears from the text.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:13:1)


**וָאֵצְאָה בְשַׁעַר הַגַּיְא.** יָצָאתִי בְּאוֹתוֹ **שַׁעַר בַּלַּיְלָה** עִם הָאֲנָשִׁים אֲשֶׁר עִמִּי לְצַד אוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם שֶׁל **עֵין הַתַּנִּין** וּלְאוֹתוֹ **שַׁעַר הָאַשְׁפֹּת** שֶׁבְּחוֹמוֹת הָעִיר. וְהָיִיתִי פּוֹרֵץ בַּחוֹמָה, וְהַחוֹמָה הָיְתָה נוֹחָה לְהַפִּילָהּ וְלִפְרֹץ בָּהּ לְפִי שֶׁהָיְתָה שְׂרוּפָה **בָאֵשׁ**:


**And I went out through the Gate of the Valley** I went out through that gate at night with the men who were with me toward En Hattannin and the Dung Gate in the wall of the city; and I broke the wall, and the wall was easily cast down and broken because it had been burned with fire.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:13:2)


**אֲשֶׁר הֵם פְּרוּצִים.** שֶׁהֵם אוֹתָם מְקוֹמוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ פְּרוּצִים וְהֵם פָּרְצוּ בָּהֶם יוֹתֵר:


**which were breached** which are those places that were breached, and they breached them more.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:14:1)


**וָאֶעֱבֹר אֶל שַׁעַר הָעָיִן.** וְרָצִיתִי לַעֲבֹר אֶל אוֹתָן מְקוֹמוֹת שֶׁבְּחוֹמַת הָעִיר וּבְאוֹתָן פְּרָצוֹת, וְאֵין מָקוֹם בְּאוֹתָן פְּרָצוֹת לַעֲבֹר בֵּינֵיהֶם רוֹכֵב עַל הַבְּהֵמוֹת, שֶׁהָיוּ פְּרָצוֹת קְטַנּוֹת:


**And I passed to the Fountain Gate** And I wished to pass to those places in the wall of the city and in those breaches, and there was no place in those breaches for riders on animals to pass through because they were so small.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:15:1)


**וָאֱהִי עוֹלֶה.** וְהָיִיתִי עוֹלֶה מִצַּד אֶחָד בַּלַּיְלָה, וְהָיִיתִי שׁוֹבֵר בַּחוֹמָה עִם הָאֲנָשִׁים אֲשֶׁר עִמִּי:


**And I ascended** And I was ascending from one side at night, and I was breaking the wall with the men who were with me.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:15:2)


**וָאָשׁוּב וָאָבוֹא.** וְכַאֲשֶׁר חָזַרְתִּי בָּאתִי דֶּרֶךְ **שַׁעַר הַגַּיְא**:


**and I returned and came** And when I returned, I came by way of the Gate of the Valley.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:16:1)


**וְהַסְּגָנִים.** אֲשֶׁר בָּעִיר, לֹא הָיוּ יוֹדְעִין לְהֵיכָן הָלַכְתִּי וְאֶת הַמַּעֲשֶׂה אֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתִי׃


**And the prefects** who were in the city did not know where I had gone to and what I had done.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:16:2)


**עַד כֵּן.** עַד עַתָּה:


**yet** Heb. עַד כֵּן, until now.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:17:1)


**וָאוֹמַר אֲלֵיהֶם.** לְמָחָר אָמַרְתִּי לָהֶם:


**And I said to them** On the morrow I said to them.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:17:2)


**וְלֹא נִהְיֶה עוֹד חֶרְפָּה.** שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ צָרֵינוּ בּוֹזְזִים וְשׁוֹלְלִים אוֹתָנוּ:


**and we shall no longer be a reproach** that our adversaries should not plunder and pillage us.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:18:1)


**אֶת יַד אֱלֹהַי.** אֲשֶׁר נָתַן לִי חֵן וְהַצְלָחָה לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**the hand of my God** who gave me favor and success before the king.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:18:2)


**לַטּוֹבָה.** הַכֹּל נִתְכַּוְּנוּ לְטוֹבָה, לִבְנוֹת חוֹמַת הָעִיר וּמִגְדָּלֶיהָ:


**for good** They intended everything for good—to build the wall of the city and its towers.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:19:1)


**וַיַּלְעִיגוּ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר שָׁמְעוּ הַצָּרִים שְׁמוּעַת בִּנְיַן חוֹמַת הָעִיר, הָיוּ מַלְעִיגִים עָלֵינוּ:


**and they mocked** When our adversaries heard the news of the building of the walls of the city, they mocked us.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:19:2)


**הֲעַל הַמֶּלֶךְ.** אִם עַל הַמֶּלֶךְ אַתֶּם מוֹרְדִים לִבְנוֹת חוֹמַת הָעִיר כְּדֵי לִמְרוֹד בּוֹ:


**are...against the king** Are you rebelling against the king by building the wall of the city in order to rebel against him?



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 2:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/2:20:1)


**וְלָכֶם אֵין חֵלֶק.** מַה לָּכֶם בְּבִנְיַן חוֹמַת הָעִיר הַזֹּאת כִּי אֵין לָכֶם חֵלֶק בָּהּ:


**but you have no share** What do you have in the building of the wall of this city, for you have no share in it.



## Chapter 3


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:1:1)


**שַׁעַר הַצֹּאן.** כָּךְ שְׁמוֹ שֶׁל שַׁעַר:


**the Sheep Gate** Such was the name of the gate.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:1:2)


**הֵמָּה קִדְּשׁוּהוּ.** לְאוֹתוֹ שַׁעַר בִּקְדֻשַּׁת הָעִיר:


**they hallowed it** that gate with the sanctity of the city.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:1:3)


**דַּלְתוֹתָיו.** שֶׁל שַׁעַר:


**its doors** i.e., of the gate.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:1:4)


**וְעַד מִגְדַּל הַמֵּאָה.** כָּךְ שְׁמוֹ שֶׁל מִגְדָּל:


**and until the Tower of the Hundred** Such was the name of the tower.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:2:1)


**וְעַל יָדוֹ.** אֵצֶל יָדוֹ, בְּסָמוּךְ לוֹ שֶׁל כָּל בּוֹנֶה וּבוֹנֶה שֶׁבָּנוּ אוֹתוֹ בְּבִנְיָן שֶׁל שַׁעַר הַצֹּאן:


**And next to it** Next to it, near the Sheep’s Gate, each builder built.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:2:2)


**בָּנוּ אַנְשֵׁי יְרֵחוֹ.** שֶׁכָּךְ הָיוּ מִנְהָגָם: הַלָּלוּ הָיוּ בּוֹנִים כָּאן בְּחוֹמַת הָעִיר עַד תַּשְׁלוּם בִּנְיָנָהּ הַמּוּטָל עֲלֵיהֶם, וְהָאֲחֵרִים הָיוּ בּוֹנִים סָמוּךְ לַבִּנְיָן הָרִאשׁוֹן, וְכָךְ הָיָה מִנְהָגָם בָּזֶה אַחַר זֶה עַד שֶׁנִּבְנֵית כָּל הַחוֹמָה:


**the men of Jericho built** Such was their custom, these would build here [their section] of the wall of the city until [the part] they were responsible for was completed, and then others would build next to the first construction. Such was their custom, one after another, until the entire wall was built.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:2:3)


**וְעַל.** כְּמוֹ (במדבר ב:כ) ״וְעָלָיו מַטֵּה מְנַשֶּׁה״, בְּסָמוּךְ, וְכֵן פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: סָמוּךְ לְבִנְיָן יָדוֹ. וְהַמִּקְרָא לֹא דִקְדֵּק בִּלְשׁוֹנוֹ, פְּעָמִים שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר: **עַל יָדוֹ**, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר: עָלָיו, וְאַחֲרָיו הֶחֱזִיק:


**And next** As in (Num. 2:20) “Next to him, the tribe of Manasseh”, near him, and such is its explanation, next to the construction, and Scripture was not particular in its choice of words. Sometimes it says ודי לע, and sometimes it says וילו. “And next to them he repaired.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:3:1)


**וְאֶת שַׁעַר הַדָּגִים.** כָּךְ שֵׁם הַשַּׁעַר:


**the Fish Gate** Such was the name of the gate.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:3:2)


**הֵמָּה קֵרוּהוּ.** נָתְנוּ הַקּוֹרוֹת וְתִקְרוֹת עַל הַשַּׁעַר:


**they laid its beams** They placed the beams and ceilings on the gate.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:3:3)


**קֵרוּהוּ.** מִשְׁקָל חָזָק, כְּמוֹ מִן קָרָה, דּוּגְמַת ״הַמְקָרֶה בַמַּיִם עֲלִיּוֹתָיו״ (תהילים קד:ג). וְקוּ״ף שֶׁל קֵרוּהוּ אֵינָהּ נְהוּגָה לִינָּקֵד בְּחִירִ״ק בְּעִנְיָן זֶה מִפְּנֵי רֵי״שׁ שֶׁל אַחֲרֶיהָ, כְּמוֹ ״בֵּרַךְ״ (תהילים י:ג), ״חֵרֵף עֹשֵׂהוּ״ (משלי יז:ה), ״וּבֵרַכְתִּיהָ וְהָיְתָה לְגוֹיִם״ (בראשית יז:טז), ״וְזֵרִיתִים בָּאֲרָצוֹת״ (יחזקאל כט:יב):


**they laid its beams** Heb. קֵֵֵֵרוּהוּ the intensive conjugation, like “...behold he laid the beams (קֵרָה),” [which appears nowhere in Scripture;] similar to (Ps. 104:3): “Who roofs (הַמְקָרֶה) His upper chambers with water.” The “kuf” of קֵרוּהוּ is not customarily vowelized with a “hirik” in this context because of the “resh” following it, like (ibid. 10:3): “blessed (בֵּרֵךְ)”; (Prov. 17: 5): “blasphemed (חֵרֵף)”; (Gen. 17:16): “I shall bless her (וּבֵרַכְתִּיהָ)”;(Ezek. 29:12): “and I shall scatter them (וּזְרֵתִים) in the lands.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:3:4)


**מַנְעוּלָיו.** מַסְגְּרָיו שֶׁשְׁוִירְלָ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**its locks** מַנְעוּלָיו, ses verrous in French, its bolts (locks).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:4:1)


**הֶחֱזִיק.** לִבְנוֹת הַבִּנְיָן:


**repaired** Heb. הֶחְֱזִיק, [lit. strengthened,] to build the building.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:5:1)


**הַתְּקוֹעִים.** כָּךְ הָיוּ נִקְרָאִים עַל שֵׁם מְקוֹמָם שֶׁהָיָה תְּקוֹעַ:


**the Tekoites** They were called this because of their place, which was Tekoa.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:5:2)


**אַדִּירֵיהֶם**. עֲשִׁירִים שֶׁל תְּקוֹעַ:


**but their mighty men** the wealthy men of Tekoa.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:5:3)


**לֹא הֵבִיאוּ צַוָּרָם.** לֹא סִיְּעוּ בַּעֲבוֹדָתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, לִבְנוֹת חוֹמַת הָעִיר:


**did not bring their neck** They did not assist in the service of the Holy One, blessed be He, to build the wall of the city.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:6:1)


**וְאֶת שַׁעַר הַיְשָׁנָה.** כָּךְ שְׁמָהּ:


**And...the gate of the old [city]** Heb. הַיְשָּׁנָה, the Gate Yeshana, such was its name.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:7:1)


**לְכִסֵּא פַּחַת עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר.** כִּסֵּא אֶחָד בָּנָה כְּדֵי לֵישֵׁב בּוֹ אוֹתוֹ פַּחַת שֶׁל עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר:


**a throne for the governor of the land beyond the river** The [people of] the land beyond the river built a throne for the governor of the land beyond the river to sit upon.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:8:1)


**צוֹרְפִים.** אוֹתָהּ מִשְׁפָּחָה הָיְתָה נִקְרֵאת עַל שֵׁם אוּמָּנוּתָם:


**goldsmiths** That family was called by their craft.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:8:2)


**וַיַּעַזְבוּ יְרוּשָׁלִַם.** מִלְּאוּהָ עָפָר עַד הַחוֹמָה הָרְחָבָה, כְּדֵי לְהַחֲזִיקָהּ:


**and they filled Jerusalem** Heb. וַיַעַזְבוּ יְרוּשָּׁלַיִם They filled it with earth up to the wide wall in order to strengthen it.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:9:1)


**שַׂר חֲצִי פֶּלֶךְ יְרוּשָׁלִָם.** שַׂר הָיָה עַל חֲצִי הָעָם שֶׁבִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, פֶּלֶךְ לְשׁוֹן דֻּכָּסוּת וּמַלְכוּת:


**the ruler of half the district of Jerusalem** He was the prince over half the people in Jerusalem. פֶּלֶךְ is an expression of a duchy or a kingdom.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:10:1)


**וְנֶגֶד בֵּיתוֹ.** נָפַל חֶלְקוֹ לִבְנוֹת שֶׁהָיָה בֵּיתוֹ סָמוּךְ לְאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם שֶׁל חוֹמַת הָעִיר:


**and opposite his house** his lot fell to build, for his house was near that place of the wall of the city.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:11:1)


**מִדָּה שֵׁנִית.** כְּאוֹתָהּ מִדָּה אֲשֶׁר בָּנָה חֲבֵירוֹ שֶׁל מַעְלָה, גַּם הוּא בָּנָה כְּמוֹתוֹ, **מִדָּה שֵׁנִית** בְּבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה. אֵינוֹ נוֹפֵל לוֹמַר לְשׁוֹן מִדָּה בְּבִנְיַן הַשְּׁעָרִים, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם כִּי אִם בְּבִנְיַן חֵלֶק שֶׁל חוֹמָה, שֶׁלֹּא מָצִינוּ בְּכָל הַפָּרָשָׁה בְּבִנְיַן הַשְּׁעָרִים לְשׁוֹן מִדָּה:


**a second measure** Just like the measurement which his friend built before [him], so he also built like [his friend.] **a second measure** in the building of the wall. The expression of measure is inappropriate for the building of the gates because they have [a measure] only in the building of a part of the wall, for we do not find in the entire chapter of the building of the gates the expression of a measure.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:11:2)


**וְאֶת מִגְדַּל הַתַּנּוּרִים.** גַּם הוּא בָּנָה אוֹתוֹ מִגְדָּל שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ כָּךְ. כָּל הַמִּגְדָּלִים הַלָּלוּ שֶׁבַּפָּרָשָׁה הָיוּ בְנוּיִים בְּחוֹמַת הָעִיר סָבִיב:


**the Tower of the Furnaces** He also built a tower by that name. All these towers in the chapter were built in the wall around the city.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:12:1)


**הוּא וּבְנוֹתָיו.** שַׁלּוּם וּבְנוֹתָיו הֶחֱזִיקוּ לִבְנוֹת הַבִּנְיָן:


**he and his daughters** Shallum and his daughters repaired the building of the structure.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:13:1)


**עַד שַׁעַר הַשְּׁפוֹת.** וְשַׁעַר **הָאַשְׁפּוֹת** לֹא הָיָה בִּכְלָל:


**until the Dung Gate** but the Dung Gate was not included.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:1)


**שַׂר פֶּלֶךְ הַמִּצְפָּה.** שַׂר וְדֻוכּוֹס הַמִּצְפָּה:


**the ruler of the district of Mizpah** the prince and duke of Mizpah.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:2)


**הוּא יִבְנֶנּוּ.** הוּא הָיָה בּוֹנֶה אוֹתוֹ שַׁעַר:


**he built it** he was building the same gate.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:3)


**וַיְטַלְלֶנּוּ.** וְהוּא הָיָה מְסַכֵּךְ אוֹתוֹ, לְשׁוֹן מְטַלַלְתָּא:


**and roofed it** And he laid the roof on it, an expression of a ceiling (מְטַלַלְתָּא).


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:4)


**וְאֶת חוֹמַת בְּרֵכַת הַשֶּׁלַח.** שֶׁהוּא נוֹטֶה לְצַד גַּן הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**and the wall of the pool of Shelah** which inclines towards the king’s garden.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:15:5)


**וְעַד הַמַּעֲלוֹת.** הֵם הָיוּ מַכִּירִים מְקוֹמוֹת הַלָּלוּ:


**and until the steps** They recognized these places.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:16:1)


**בֵּית צוּר**. שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


**Beth Zur** the name of a place.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:17:1)


**לְפִלְכּוֹ.** מֵאוֹתָהּ שְׂרָרָה חֲצִי פֶלֶךְ בָּנָה בִּנְיָן זֶה. לְפִלְכּוֹ כָּ״ף דְּגוּשָׁה, כַּאֲשֶׁר יֹאמַר מִן מֶלֶךְ מַלְכּוֹ, מִן עֶבֶד עַבְדּוֹ, כֵּן יֹאמַר מִן פֶּלֶךְ פִּלְכּוֹ; שֶׁאוֹת אַחֲרוֹנָה שֶׁל שֵׁם דָּבָר מֵאוֹתִיּוֹת בג״ד כפ״ת דְּגוּשׁוֹת:


**half the district** From that bailiwick, the half district, he built this structure. In the word לְפִּלְכּוֹ, the “kaff” has a “dagesh,” as one says from מֶלֶךְ, מַלְכּוֹ, from עֶבֶד, עַבְדוֹ, so does one say from פֶּלֶךְ, פִּלְכּוֹ, for [if] the last letter of the noun is one of the letters “beth,” “gimmel,” “daleth,” “kaff,” “pey,” or “tav,” it is punctuated with a “dagesh.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:18:1)


**אֲחֵיהֶם**. הַלְוִיִּם:


**their brothers** the Levites.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:19:1)


**מִנֶּגֶד עֲלוֹת הַנֶּשֶׁק.** כְּנֶגֶד אוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹלִים הַגִּבּוֹרִים אֶל **הַנֶּשֶׁק**. לְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ שֶׁל מִקְרָא נִרְאֶה שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם מָקוֹם אֶחָד בְּסָמוּךְ לַחוֹמָה וּכְלֵי זַיִן שֶׁל נֶשֶׁק הַגִּבּוֹרִים הָיוּ נוֹתְנִין (ספרים אחרים: נְתוּנִים) שָׁם כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״עָרֵי מִבְצָר וְהַנֶּשֶׁק״ (מלכים ב י:ב):


**opposite the ascent to the armory** opposite the place where the mighty men would ascend to the weapons. According to the simple interpretation of the verse, it appears that there was a place near the wall where the mighty men kept their weapons, as it is stated: (II Kings 10:2) “...and cities (sic) of fortification and arms.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:19:2)


**הַמִּקְצוֹעַ.** לְקֶרֶן זָוִית שֶׁל חוֹמָה׃


**at the corner** at the corner of the wall.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:20:1)


**הֶחֱרָה.** פִּתְרוֹנוֹ לְפִי עִנְיָנוֹ, וְכֶפֶל לָשׁוֹן הוּא עַל הַחִזּוּק:


**repaired** Heb. הֶחֱרָה. Its interpretation is according to the context, and it is a double expression for repairing.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:21:1)


**מִפֶּתַח בֵּית אֶלְיָשִׁיב וְעַד תַּכְלִית וְגוֹ׳.** מִנֶּגֶד אוֹתוֹ בַּיִת בָּנָה בִּנְיָנוֹ:


**from the entrance of the house of Eliashib until the end of, etc.** Opposite that house he built his structure.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:23:1)


**נֶגֶד בֵּיתָם.** שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם בְּסָמוּךְ לַחוֹמָה:


**opposite their house** which was there near the wall.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:25:1)


**וְהַמִּגְדָּל הַיּוֹצֵא.** מִגְדָּל אֶחָד הָיָה בַּחוֹמָה שֶׁהוּא יוֹצֵא וּבוֹלֵט מִבִּפְנִים:


**and the tower that juts out** There was one tower in the wall that jutted out and protruded from within.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:26:1)


**וְהַנְּתִינִים הָיוּ יֹשְׁבִים בָּעֹפֶל.** וְהַנְּתִינִים הָיוּ יוֹשְׁבִים בְּאוֹתוֹ מָחוֹז, וְהָיוּ בּוֹנִים אוֹתוֹ מָחוֹז שֶׁלָּהֶם שֶׁהָיָה סָמוּךְ לַחוֹמָה עַד נֶגֶד שַׁעַר הַמַּיִם שֶׁל צַד הַמִּזְרָח:


**And the Nethinites dwelt in the Ophel** And the Nethinites were dwelling in that district, and they were building their district, which was near the wall, until opposite the Water Gate of the eastern side.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:28:1)


**אִישׁ לְנֶגֶד בֵּיתוֹ.** כָּל אֶחָד הָיָה בּוֹנֶה כְּנֶגֶד בֵּיתוֹ:


**each one opposite his house** Each one was building opposite his house.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:30:1)


**וְחָנוּן בֶּן צָלָף הַשִּׁשִּׁי.** בָּנִים הַרְבֵּה הָיוּ לוֹ לְצָלָף, וְחָנוּן הוּא **הַשִּׁשִּׁי**:


**and Hanun the sixth son of Zalaph** Zalaph had many sons, and Hanun was the sixth.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:30:2)


**מִדָּה שֵׁנִי.** כְּמוֹ ״שֵׁנִית״ (נחמיה ג:כז):


**a second measure** Heb. מִדָה שֵּׁנִי, like שֵּׁנִית.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:30:3)


**נִשְׁכָּתוֹ.** כְּמוֹ לִשְׁכָּתוֹ, לִשְׁכָּה אַחַת הָיְתָה לוֹ שָׁם סָמוּךְ לַחוֹמָה:


**his chamber** Heb. נִשְּׁכָּתוֹ, like לִשְּׁכָּתוֹ. He had one chamber adjoining the wall.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:31:1)


**בֶּן הַצּוֹרְפִי.** אִישׁ הַצּוֹרֵף, וְנִקְרָא שֶׁל שֵׁם אוּמָנוּתוֹ:


**the son of Hatzorefi** the goldsmith, and he was named after his craft.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:32:1)


**וּבֵין עֲלִיַּת הַפִּנָּה לְשַׁעַר הַצֹּאן.** תְּחִלַּת הַבִּנְיָן הִתְחִילוּ לִבְנוֹת שַׁעַר הַצֹּאן, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיִּבְנוּ אֶת שַׁעַר הַצֹּאן וְגוֹ׳״ (נחמיה ג:א). כָּךְ הָיוּ בּוֹנִין הַחוֹמָה מִסָּבִיב בָּזֶה אַחַר זֶה:


**And between the ascent to the tower and the Sheep Gate** At the beginning of the building they began to build the Sheep Gate, as it says (verse 1): “...and they built the Sheep Gate, etc.” So did they build the wall all around, one after the other.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:1)


**לִפְנֵי אֶחָיו וְחֵיל שֹׁמְרוֹן.** לִפְנֵי חֲבֵירָיו וְחֵיל שֹׁמְרוֹן שֶׁהָיוּ צָרֵי יְהוּדָה:


**before his brethren and the army of Samaria** before his brethren and the army of Samaria, who were the adversaries of Judea.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:2)


**מָה הַיְּהוּדִים.** מַדּוּעַ הַיְּהוּדִים כָּךְ הֵם עוֹשִׂים?:


**What are the Jews** Why are the Jews doing this?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:3)


**הָאֲמֵלָלִים.** עִנְיַן חֲלָשׁוּת וְרִפְיוֹן:


**the feeble** Heb. הָאֲמֵלָלִִים, a term denoting weakness and feebleness.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:4)


**הֲיַעַזְבוּ לָהֶם.** וְכִי הֵם סְבוּרִים אֲשֶׁר יַעַזְבוּ וְיַנִּיחוּ לָהֶם הָעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים לִבְנוֹת?:


**Will they let them** Do they think that the heathens will allow them to build?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:34:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:5)


**הֲיִזְבָּחוּ.** אִם יִזְבְּחוּ לָהֶם אֶת זִבְחֵיהֶם תָּמִיד כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֵם סְבוּרִים:


**Will they sacrifice** Will they constantly slaughter their sacrifices for themselves as they think?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:34:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:6)


**הַיְכַלּוּ בַיּוֹם.** אִם יְכַלּוּ וְיַשְׁלִימוּ מְלַאכְתָּם אֲשֶׁר הִתְחִילוּ בְּיוֹם א׳? לוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא יַנִּיחוּם הָעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים לִגְמֹר אֶת בִּנְיָנָם:


**Will they finish in one day** Will they complete the work that they started in one day? That is to say that the heathens will not permit them to complete their building.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:34:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:7)


**הֲיְחַיּוּ.** אִם יְחַיּוּ וִיתַקְּנוּ וְיַחֲזִירוּ **הָאֲבָנִים** לְקַדְמָתָן לִהְיוֹת קָשׁוֹת וַחֲזָקוֹת מִן **הֶעָפָר**:


**Will they revive** Will they revive and repair and restore stones to their previous state to be harder and stronger than the dust?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:34:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:8)


**מֵעֲרֵמוֹת.** עִנְיַן חֳמָרִים וְתִלִּים:


**from the heaps** Heb. מֵעֲרֵמוֹת, a term denoting heaps and mounds.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:34:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:34:9)


**וְהֵמָּה שְׂרוּפוֹת.** וְהָאֲבָנִים הָיוּ שְׂרוּפוֹת וְנַעֲשֵׂית עָפָר. כָּךְ הָיוּ מַלְעִיגִים וּמִתְלוֹצְצִים עַל בִּנְיַן הַחוֹמָה, שֶׁהָיוּ בּוֹנִין מִן הָאֲבָנִים שְׂרוּפוֹת שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָאָרֶץ מִן הָאֵשׁ כַּאֲשֶׁר נִשְׂרְפָה יְרוּשָׁלָיִם:


**for they are burnt** The stones were burnt and have become dust. So did they jeer and ridicule the building of the wall, which they were building from the burnt stones that had fallen to the earth from the fire when Jerusalem was burned.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:35:1)


**וְטוֹבִיָּה הָעַמּוֹנִי אֶצְלוֹ.** טוֹבִיָּה הָיָה אֶצְלוֹ שֶׁל סַנְבַלָּט:


**And Tobiah the Ammonite was beside him** Tobiah was beside Sanballat.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:35:2)


**וַיֹּאמֶר.** וְכָךְ הָיָה אוֹמֵר וּמַלְעִיג:


**and he said** And so he was saying and ridiculing.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:35:3)


**גַּם אֲשֶׁר הֵם בּוֹנִים.** אַף הַבִּנְיָן הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר הֵם בּוֹנִים:


**Even what they build** even this building that they are building.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:35:4)


**אִם יַעֲלֶה שׁוּעָל.** עַל הַבִּנְיָן לִפְרֹץ חוֹמַת הָעִיר, מַה יְּהֵא עָלָיו? לוֹמַר שֶׁאֵין לַבִּנְיָן תַּקָּנָה, וְכָל מִי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה יָבֹא וְיִפְרֹץ בּוֹ:


**if a fox comes up** on the building to breach the wall of the city, what will become of it? That is to say that the building cannot be repaired, and whoever wishes, can come and break into it.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:36:1)


**שְׁמַע אֱלֹהֵינוּ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר שָׁמַעְתִּי בְּלַעֲגָם עָלֵינוּ הִתְפַּלַּלְתִּי תְּפִלָּה זֹאת:


**Hearken, our God** When I heard their ridicule of us, I prayed this prayer.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:36:2)


**בְּאֶרֶץ שִׁבְיָה.** שֶׁיִּגְלוּ מֵעַל אַדְמָתָם כַּאֲשֶׁר גָּלִינוּ:


**in a land of captivity** that they be exiled from the land as we were exiled.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:37:1)


**וְאַל תְּכַס.** כְּמוֹ וְאַל תְּכַסֶּה, דּוּגְמַת ״וְסוֹד אַחֵר אַל תְּגַל״ (משלי כה:ט), כְּמוֹ אַל תְּגַלֶּה:


**And do not conceal** Heb. וְאַל תְּכַס, like וְאַל תְּכַסֶה, similar to (Prov. 25:9): “...but do not divulge (תְּגָל) another’s secret,” like תְּגַלֶה.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:37:2)


**כִּי הִכְעִיסוּ.** אֲשֶׁר הִכְעִיסוּ נֶגֶד הַבּוֹנִים לְהַלְעִיגָם:


**for they have provoked** that they have provoked the builders to ridicule them.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 3:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/3:38:1)


**וַתִּקָּשֵׁר.** כַּאֲשֶׁר נִתְקַשְּׁרָה הַחוֹמָה וְנִבְנֵית עַד חֶצְיָהּ, אָז הָיָה לֵב הָעָם לָהוּט וּמָשׁוּךְ לַעֲשׂוֹת בִּנְיַן הַחוֹמָה:


**was built** Heb. וַתִּקָשֵּׁר, lit. it was bound. When the wall was bound up and built to its half, the people were eager and anxious to complete their building of it.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:1:1)


**כִּי עָלְתָה אֲרוּכָה.** שֶׁנִּבְנְתָה הַחוֹמָה:


**was repaired** Heb. עָלְתָה אָרוּכָה, [lit. attained a cure,] that the wall was built.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:1:2)


**אֲרוּכָה.** עִנְיַן חוֹבֶשׁ וּתְרוּפָה:


**repaired** A [word having the] meaning of bandaging and healing.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:1:3)


**כִּי הֵחֵלּוּ הַפְּרוּצִים לְהִסָּתֵם.** אֲשֶׁר הִתְחִילוּ אַנְשֵׁי הָעִיר שֶׁהָיוּ פְּרוּצִים עַד עַתָּה מֵאֵין חוֹמָה הָיוּ עַתָּה סְתוּמִים בְּבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה:


**that the people who were exposed had commenced to be closed in** that the people of the city, who had heretofore been exposed without a wall, were now closed in by the building of the wall.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:2:1)


**וְלַעֲשׂוֹת לוֹ תּוֹעָה.** לַעֲשׂוֹת לוֹ בְּבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה קִלְקוּל וְהַשְׁחָתָה. תּוֹעָה כְּמוֹ ״וּלְדַבֵּר אֶל ה׳ תּוֹעָה״ (ישעיהו לב:ו):


**and to wreak destruction therein** to wreak upon the building of the wall ruin and destruction. It is like (Isa. 32:5): “...and to speak destructively (תּוֹעָה) about the Lord.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:3:1)


**עֲלֵיהֶם.** עַל הַבּוֹנִים:


**over them** over the builders.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:3:2)


**מִפְּנֵיהֶם.** מִפְּנֵי הָאוֹיְבִים:


**because of them** because of the enemies.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:4:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר יְהוּדָה.** כָּךְ הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים וּמִתְאוֹנְנִים אַנְשֵׁי יְהוּדָה:


**And Judea said** So were the people of Judea saying and complaining.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:4:2)


**כָּשַׁל כֹּחַ הַסַּבָּל.** הַסּוֹבְלִים וְעוֹמְסִים הָאֲבָנִים וְהָעֵצִים לְבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה נִכְשְׁלוּ בְכֹחָם, שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם כֹּחַ לִבְנוֹת מִפְּנֵי הָאוֹיְבִים:


**The porter’s strength has failed** Those who bear and load the stones and the wood for the building of the wall have failed in their strength, for they have no more strength to build because of the enemies.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:4:3)


**וְעָפָר הַרְבֵּה.** עֲדַיִן יֵשׁ טִיט וְחֹמֶר הַרְבֵּה לִיתֵּן בְּבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה, לוֹמַר שֶׁעֲדַיִן הַמְּלָאכָה גְּדוֹלָה עָלֵינוּ לִבְנוֹת, וַאֲנַחְנוּ לֹא נוּכַל לִבְנוֹת בַּחוֹמָה מִפְּנֵי פַּחַד הָאוֹיְבִים:


**and there is much earth** There is still much mud and clay to place into the wall, meaning that we still have much work, but we cannot build because of fear of the enemies.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:5:1)


**וַיֹּאמְרוּ צָרֵינוּ.** כָּךְ הָיָה נְחֶמְיָה מְסַפֵּר וְהוֹלֵךְ:


**And our adversaries said** Nehemiah proceeds to tell this.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:5:2)


**לֹא יֵדְעוּ וְלֹא יִרְאוּ.** כָּךְ הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים צָרֵינוּ עָלֵינוּ: **לֹא יֵדְעוּ וְלֹא** יַכִּירוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בָּנוּ **עַד אֲשֶׁר נָבוֹא** פִּתְאוֹם **לְתוֹכָם** וְנַהֲרֹג אוֹתָם, וּבְדָבָר זֶה נִשְׁבֹּת וּנְבַטֵּל הַבִּנְיָן:


**They will not know, and they will not see** So were our adversaries saying about us: “They will not know, and the Israelites will not recognize us until we come suddenly into their midst and kill them, and with this plot we will stop and curtail the building.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:6:1)


**וַיְהִי כַּאֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ הַיְּהוּדִים.** וְעַתָּה נֶאֶסְפוּ הַצָּרִים אֲשֶׁר נִתְקַשְּׁרוּ יַחַד לָבֹא לְהִלָּחֵם בָּנוּ עִם הַיְּהוּדִים אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ בֵּינוֹתָם בְּאַרְצוֹתָם, וְאוֹתָם הַיְּהוּדִים אֲשֶׁר נִתְחַבְּרוּ עִמָּהֶם בָּאוּ לִפְנֵי הַחַיִל כְּדֶרֶךְ אַנְשֵׁי הַמִּלְחָמָה לְדַבֵּר אֵלֵינוּ. וּבָעִנְיָן הַזֶּה הֶעֱרִימוּ הַיְּהוּדִים אֶת הָאוֹיְבִים **וַיְהִי כַּאֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ הַיְּהוּדִים הַיּוֹשְׁבִים אֶצְלָם וְגוֹ׳**:


**Now it came to pass when the Jews came** And now, the adversaries who had conspired together to wage war against us gathered with the Jews who were among them in their lands, and those Jews who had joined them came before the army, as is customary for soldiers to do, to speak to us, and in this manner the Jews conspired against the enemies. Now it came to pass when the Jews who dwelt beside them came, etc.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:6:2)


**וַיֹּאמְרוּ לָנוּ עֶשֶׂר פְּעָמִים.** הִתְרוּ בָּנוּ אוֹתָם הַיְּהוּדִים עַד עֶשֶׂר פְּעָמִים לְהִלָּחֵם בְּאוֹיְבֵינוּ כִּי הֵם בָּאִים לַמִּלְחָמָה, וְכָךְ אָמְרוּ לָנוּ: **מִכָּל הַמְּקוֹמוֹת וְגוֹ׳** מִכָּל אוֹתָן הַמְּקוֹמוֹת שֶׁתִּהְיוּ נְפוֹצִים שָׁם בְּבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה אָנָה וָאָנָה, תִּהְיוּ זְהִירִים לִהְיוֹת נֶאֱסָפִים יַחַד עַד אֲשֶׁר תָּשׁוּבוּ עָלֵינוּ בְּיַחַד לְהִלָּחֵם בָּנוּ:


**that they said to us ten times** Those Jews warned us as many as ten times to wage war against our enemies because they were coming to war. And this is what they said to us, “From all the places, etc.” From all those places where you will be scattered while building the wall, here and there, be careful to assemble until you return upon us together to wage war against us.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:7:1)


**וָאַעֲמִיד מִתַּחְתִּיּוֹת.** לַחוֹמָה, כַּאֲשֶׁר שָׁמַעְתִּי דִּבְרֵיהֶם הֶעֱמַדְתִּי מִתַּחְתִּיּוֹת לְאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם סָבִיב לִירוּשָׁלַיִם מֵאֲחוֹרֵי לְחוֹמַת הָעִיר בַּצְּחִיחִים:


**So I stationed from below...the wall** When I heard their words, I stationed [mighty men] from below that place around Jerusalem, from behind the wall of the city and on the bare rocks.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:7:2)


**לְמִשְׁפָּחוֹת.** מִן הָעָם מִגִּבּוֹרִים שֶׁבָּהֶם, עִם כְּלֵי זַיִן שֶׁבָּהֶם, לְהִלָּחֵם בְּאוֹיְבֵינוּ כַּאֲשֶׁר יָבֹאוּ:


**according to families** of the people, of the mighty men among them with their weapons, to fight against our enemies when they would come.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:7:3)


**וָאַעֲמִיד מִתַּחְתִּיּוֹת וְגוֹ׳ וָאַעֲמִיד אֶת הָעָם וְגוֹ׳.** מִקְרָא זֶה דּוּגְמַת ״וְהָיוּ הַמַּיִם אֲשֶׁר תִּקַּח מִן הַיְאֹר וְגוֹ׳ וְהָיוּ לְדָם בַּיַּבָּשֶׁת״ (שמות ד:ט), ״כִּי הִנֵּה אוֹיְבֶיךָ ה׳ כִּי הִנֵּה אוֹיְבֶיךָ יֹאבֵדוּ״ (תהלים צב:י), ״עוּרִי עוּרִי דְּבוֹרָה עוּרִי עוּרִי דַּבְּרִי שִׁיר״ (שופטים ה:יב), ״לֹא לָנוּ ה׳ לֹא לָנוּ״ (תהלים קטו:א):


**And I stationed from below, etc. and I stationed the people, etc.** This verse follows the pattern of (Exod. 4:9): “...and the water that you will take from the river will be...will become blood on the dry land;” (Ps. 92:10): “For behold, Your enemies, O Lord, for behold, Your enemies shall perish;” (Jud. 5:12): “Praise! Praise! Deborah. Praise! Praise! Utter a song;” (Ps. 115:1): “Not to us, O Lord, not to us.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:8:1)


**וָאֵרֶא וָאָקוּם.** וְכַאֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי הָאוֹיְבִים בָּאִים, קַמְתִּי לְדַבֵּר אֶל הַחוֹרִים וְהַסְּגָנִים לְזָרְזָם לְהִלָּחֵם בְּאוֹיְבֵינוּ:


**And I saw, and I arose** And when I saw the enemies coming, I arose to speak to the nobles and to the prefects to spur them to fight against our enemy.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:8:2)


**עַל אֲחֵיכֶם.** בִּשְׁבִיל אֲחֵיכֶם:


**for your brethren** Heb. עַל אֲחֵיכֶם, for the sake of your brethren.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:9:1)


**כִּי נוֹדַע לָנוּ.** הַדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ לְהִלָּחֵם בָּנוּ:


**that it had become known to us** that they were coming to fight against us.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:9:2)


**וַנָּשָׁב כֻּלָּנוּ.** חָזַרְנוּ כֻּלָּנוּ מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱסַפְנוּ יַחַד לִקְרַאת הָאוֹיְבִים, כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד בִּמְלַאכְתּוֹ לְבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה:


**and we all returned** We all returned from the place where we had gathered to meet the enemies, each one to his work in the building of the wall.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:10:1)


**וַיְהִי מִן הַיּוֹם הַהוּא.** וּמֵאוֹתוֹ יוֹם וָהָלְאָה הָיוּ חֲצִי הַגִּבּוֹרִים עוֹשִׂים בְּבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה, וּשְׁאָר חֲצִי הַגִּבּוֹרִים הָיוּ מְזֻיָּנִים בִּכְלֵי זֵינָם לִשְׁמֹר אֲחֵיהֶם הָעוֹשִׂים בַּמְּלָאכָה:


**And it was from that day on** And from that day on, half the mighty men worked at the building of the wall, and the remaining half of the mighty men were armed with weapons to guard their brethren who were doing the work.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:10:2)


**וְהָרְמָחִים.** וָ״ו יְתֵרָה, כְּמוֹ וָ״ו שֶׁל ״וְאַיָּה וַעֲנָה״ (בראשית לו:כד):


**spears** Heb. וְהָרְמָחִים. The “vav” is superfluous, like the “vav” of (Gen. 36: 24): “And these are the sons of Zibeon: Aiah (וְאַיָה) and Anah.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:10:3)


**וְהַשָּׂרִים.** הָיוּ מְחֻבָּרִים אַחֲרֵי כָּל שֵׁבֶט יְהוּדָה, לִהְיוֹת זְהִירִים בַּמִּלְחָמָה:


**and the rulers** were close behind the entire tribe of Judah to be cautious in the battle.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:11:1)


**הַבּוֹנִים בַּחוֹמָה וְגוֹ׳.** אוֹתָם שֶׁהָיוּ בּוֹנִין וְאוֹתָן שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹמְסִים וְנוֹשְׂאִים הַמַּשָּׂאוֹת, בְּיָד אַחַת הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים מְלַאכְתָּן וּבְיָד אַחַת הָיוּ נוֹשְׂאִים כְּלֵי זַיִן:


**The builders of the wall, etc.** Those who were building the wall and those who were loading and carrying the loads were working with one hand, and carrying weapons with the other.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:11:2)


**הַשֶּׁלַח.** כְּלִי קְרָב, כְּמוֹ ״מֵעֲבוֹר בַּשֶּׁלַח״ (איוב לג:יח). וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**the sword** Heb. הַשָּׁלַח, an implement of war, like (Job 33:18): “...from perishing by the sword (מֵעֲבֹר בַּשָּׁלַח). And so did Menahem associate it (Machbereth Menahem p. 174).



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:12:1)


**וְהַבּוֹנִים.** כֵּיצַד הֵם בּוֹנִים? שֶׁהָיוּ כֻלָּם חֲגוּרִים **אִישׁ חַרְבּוֹ עַל מָתְנָיו וּבוֹנִים**:


**And the builders** How were they building? They were all girded, each one with his sword on his waist, and thus did they build.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:12:2)


**אֶצְלִי.** הוּא הָיָה תָּדִיר אֶצְלִי:


**beside me** He was always beside me.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:13:1)


**הַרְבֵּה.** הִיא גְּדוֹלָה:


**much** Heb. הַרְבֵּה, a great [amount of work].



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:14:1)


**יִלָּחֵם לָנוּ.** יִלָּחֵם בַּעֲדֵינוּ:


**will fight for us** [as translated,] will fight for us.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:16:1)


**אִישׁ וְנַעֲרוֹ.** כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד מִן הַבּוֹנִים וּבָחוּר אֶחָד עִמּוֹ:


**Let each man and his youth** each of the builders and a youth with him.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:16:2)


**יָלִינוּ בְּתוֹךְ יְרוּשָׁלִָם.** וְהַלַּיְלָה הָיָה לָהֶם לְמִשְׁמָר וְהַיּוֹם עוֹשִׂים מְלָאכָה, נִמְצָא שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ יְשֵׁנִים כְּלוּם:


**lodge in the midst of Jerusalem** And the night was for their watch, and [during] the day they were doing work. The result was that they did not sleep at all.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:17:1)


**אֵין אֲנַחְנוּ פּוֹשְׁטִים בְּגָדֵינוּ.** תָּמִיד הָיִינוּ זְרִיזִים בַּמִּשְׁמָר:


**we did not take off our clothes** We were always zealous in guarding.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:17:2)


**אִישׁ שִׁלְחוֹ הַמָּיִם.** מֵיסַב עַל **אֵין** שֶׁלְּמַעְלָה, כְּמוֹ (שמואל א ב:ג) ״יֵצֵא עָתָק מִפִּיכֶם״. אֵין אִישׁ מִמֶּנּוּ פּוֹשֵׁט בְּגָדָיו לִשְׁכַּב אוֹ לִרְחֹץ אוֹתָם בַּמַּיִם. וְכֶפֶל לָשׁוֹן הוּא עַל **אֵין אֲנַחְנוּ פוֹשְׁטִים**:


**man took it even to the water** This refers back to “not” above, like (I Sam. 2:3): “...let not arrogance come out of your mouth.” None of us would take off his clothes to lie down or to wash them with water, and this is a double expression, [repeating,] we did not undress.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 4:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/4:17:3)


**שִׁלְחוֹ.** הַפְּשָׁט כְּמוֹ ״וּפָשַׁט אֶת בְּגָדָיו״ (ויקרא ו:ד), דִּמְתֻּרְגָּם ״וְשָׁלַח״:


**took it off** Heb. שִּׁלְחוֹ, taking off, like (Lev. 6: 4): “He shall then take off his garments,” which is translated by Targum as וְיִשְּׁלַח.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:1:1)


**וַתְּהִי צַעֲקַת הָעָם וּנְשֵׁיהֶם גְּדוֹלָה.** דַּלַּת הָעָם הָעֲנִיִּים הָיוּ צוֹעֲקִים מְאֹד:


**Now the cry of the people and their wives was great** The poor people were crying exceedingly.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:1:2)


**אֶל אֲחֵיהֶם הַיְּהוּדִים.** בִּשְׁבִיל אֲחֵיהֶם הַיְּהוּדִים הָעֲשִׁירִים:


**about their brothers, the Jews** about their brothers, the rich Jews.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:2:1)


**וְיֵשׁ אֲשֶׁר אוֹמְרִים.** נוֹתֵן טַעַם לִדְבָרָיו לָמָּה הָיוּ צוֹעֲקִים: יֵשׁ מֵהֶם מִדַּלַּת הָעָם שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים **אֲנַחְנוּ רַבִּים עִם בָּנֵינוּ וּבְנוֹתֵינוּ וְנִקְחָה דָגָן** וְעַתָּה יֵשׁ לָנוּ לִמְכֹּר **בָּנֵינוּ וּבְנוֹתֵינוּ** לִיקַּח מֵהֶם **דָגָן** לְמַעַן **נִחְיֶה**. כָּךְ הָיוּ צוֹעֲקִים עַל הַיְּהוּדִים הָעֲשִׁירִים שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ רוֹצִים לְפַרְנְסָם:


**There were some who were saying** He gives a reason for his words: Why were they crying? Some of the poor people were saying, “We are many with our sons and daughters and have to buy corn. Now we will have to sell our sons and daughters in order to buy corn in order to live.” So were they crying about the rich Jews who did not wish to support them.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:3:1)


**וְיֵשׁ אֲשֶׁר אוֹמְרִים שְׂדוֹתֵינוּ וְגוֹ׳.** וְיֵשׁ מֵהֶם בָּעֲנִיִּים שֶׁהָיוּ מִתְאוֹנְנִים וְאוֹמְרִים: שָׂדוֹת וּכְרָמִים וּבָתִּים נָתַן לַאֲחֵרִים בְּעֵרָבוֹן וּמַשְׁכּוֹן לִיקַּח דָּגָן לִמְזוֹנוֹתֵינוּ:


**And there were some who were saying “Our fields, etc.”** And there were some among the poor who were complaining and saying “Our fields, vineyards and houses we shall give to others as a pledge and as security to buy corn for our food.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:3:2)


**בָּרָעָב.** אַחֲרֵי שֶׁאֵין מְוַתְּרִים לָנוּ הָעֲשִׁירִים מִנִּכְסֵיהֶם לְפַרְנָסָתֵנוּ:


**for the hunger** since the rich do not give any of their property up to us for our support.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:4:1)


**וְיֵשׁ אֲשֶׁר אוֹמְרִים לָוִינוּ כֶסֶף.** וְיֵשׁ מֵהֶם אֲשֶׁר אוֹמְרִים: כְּבָר **לָוִינוּ כֶסֶף** מֵאֲחֵרִים עַל **שְׂדוֹתֵינוּ וּכְרָמֵינוּ** לִפְרֹעַ מַס **הַמֶּלֶךְ**, וּמֵעַכְשָׁיו אֵין לָנוּ כְּלוּם לְצֹרֶךְ פַּרְנָסָתֵנוּ:


**And there were some who were saying, “We have borrowed money”** And there were some who were saying, “We have already borrowed money from others on our fields and our vineyards to pay the king’s tax, and now we have nothing for our support.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:4:2)


**לְמִדַּת הַמֶּלֶךְ.** לְמַס הַמֶּלֶךְ, כְּמוֹ ״מִנְדָּה בְלוֹ וַהֲלָךְ״ (עזרא ד:יג):


**for the king’s tax** Heb. לְמִדַּת, for the king’s tax, like (Ezra 4:13): “...the king’s due (מִנְדָּה), the head tax, and the meal tax.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:1)


**וְעַתָּה כִּבְשַׂר אַחֵינוּ בְשָׂרֵנוּ.** וְעַכְשָׁיו אָנוּ חֲשׁוּבִים וּמְיֻחָסִים כְּאַחֵינוּ שֶׁהֵם עֲשִׁירִים:


**And now, like the flesh of our brethren is our flesh** And now, we are as esteemed and distinguished as our brethren who are rich.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:2)


**כִּבְנֵיהֶם בָּנֵינוּ.** כִּבְנֵיהֶם שֶׁהֵם חֲשׁוּבִים וּמְיֻחָסִים, כְּמוֹ כֵן מְיֻחָסִים בָּנֵינוּ:


**like their children are our children** Like their children, who are esteemed and distinguished, so are our children distinguished.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:3)


**וְהִנֵּה אֲנַחְנוּ כֹבְשִׁים.** וְעַתָּה יֵשׁ לָנוּ לִהְיוֹת כֹּבְשִׁים וְנוֹתְנִים **בָּנֵינוּ וּבְנוֹתֵינוּ** לַאֲחֵרִים עַל עֵסֶק מְזוֹנוֹתֵינוּ:


**and behold, we are subjecting** And now we have to subject and deliver our sons and daughters to others for the sake of our sustenance.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:4)


**וְיֵשׁ מִבְּנוֹתֵינוּ נִכְבָּשׁוֹת.** וּכְבָר יֵשׁ קְצָת מִבְּנוֹתֵינוּ שֶׁהֵן נִכְבָּשׁוֹת לַאֲחֵרִים לְעַבְדוּת:


**and some of our daughters are subjected** And already there are some of our daughters who are subjected to others in slavery.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:5)


**וְאֵין לְאֵל יָדֵינוּ.** וְאֵין כֹּחַ בְּיָדֵינוּ לִפְדּוֹתָם:


**and we have no power** We do not have the ability to redeem them.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:5:6)


**וּשְׂדוֹתֵינוּ וּכְרָמֵינוּ לַאֲחֵרִים.** אַף שָׂדוֹת וּכְרָמִים מָסַרְנוּ בְּיַד אֲחֵרִים לְפַרְנָסָתֵנוּ, וּמַה נֹּאכַל מֵעַתָּה, כִּי אֵין לָנוּ בַּמֶּה לְהִתְפַּרְנֵס:


**and others have our fields and vineyards** We have even delivered our fields and vineyards to others for our sustenance. From now on, what shall we eat? We have nothing with which to sustain ourselves.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:7:1)


**וַיִּמָּלֵךְ.** וַיִּוָּעֵץ לִבִּי בְּעַצְמִי:


**And I thought this over to myself** Heb. וַיִמָלֵךְ לִבִּי עָלַי, and my heart took counsel within myself.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:7:2)


**וָאָרִיבָה.** נִתְוַכַּחְתִּי עִם הַחוֹרִים וְהַסְּגָנִים שֶׁהֵם הָעֲשִׁירִים:


**and I contended** I contended with the nobles and the prefects, who were the rich people.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:7:3)


**מַשָּׂא אִישׁ בְּאָחִיו אַתֶּם.** נוֹשִׁים מַדּוּעַ אַתֶּם כָּךְ עוֹשִׂים? הָיָה לָכֶם לְפַרְנֵס הָעֲנִיִּים הַלָּלוּ, וְאַתֶּם מַלְוִים לָהֶם מָמוֹן לִכְבּוֹשׁ בְּנֵיהֶם וּבְנוֹתֵיהֶם וּשְׂדוֹתֵיהֶם וְכַרְמֵיהֶם וּבָתֵּיהֶם:


**“Are you demanding each other’s loans?”** Why are you doing this? You should have supported these poor people, but you are lending them money in order to appropriate their sons and daughters, their fields, vineyards and houses.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:7:4)


**וָאֶתֵּן עֲלֵיהֶם קְהִלָּה גְדוֹלָה.** הִקְהַלְתִּי וְאָסַפְתִּי עֲלֵיהֶם קְהִלָּה גְדוֹלָה לִצְעוֹק עֲלֵיהֶם, כְּדֵי לְבַיְּשָׁם:
