**and I gathered a large assembly around them** I congregated and gathered a large assembly around them to shout at them in order to embarrass them.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:1)


**וְאָמְרָה לָהֶם.** וְכֵן אָמַרְתִּי לָהֶם: אֲנַחְנוּ קָנִינוּ כְּבָר אֶת אַחֵינוּ הַיְּהוּדִים אֲשֶׁר בַּשִּׁבְיָה מִיַּד הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ נִמְכָּרִים לָהֶם:


**And I said to them** And so I said to them: We already bought our Jewish brethren who were in captivity from the heathens to whom they were sold.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:2)


**כְּדֵי בָּנוּ.** בְּאוֹתוֹ דֵּי מָמוֹן שֶׁבְּיָדֵינוּ:


**with as much as we could afford** with as much money as was in our hands.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:3)


**וְגַם אַתֶּם.** וְאַף אַתֶּם תִּמְכְּרוּ לְעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אֶת אֲחֵיכֶם הַנִּכְבָּשִׁים תַּחַת יְדֵיכֶם לַעֲבָדִים:


**and you too** And you too will sell to the heathens your brethren who are subjected to you as slaves?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:4)


**וְנִמְכְּרוּ לָנוּ.** וְהַמַּעֲשֶׂה יָבֹא לִידֵי כָּךְ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מְכוּרִים לָנוּ מִיַּד הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת. (אִם כֵּן לָמָּה תִּמְכְּרוּ אוֹתָם כֵּיוָן שֶׁמֻּכְרָחִים אֲנַחְנוּ לִפְדּוֹתָם. כן הוא בספרים אחרים):


**and they will be sold to us** And the result will be that they will be sold to us by the heathens (and if so, why should you sell them, since we will be compelled to redeem them? This is found in certain editions).


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:8:5)


**וַיַּחֲרִישׁוּ.** שָׁתְקוּ וְנִתְבַּיְּשׁוּ וְלֹא יָדְעוּ מַה לְּהָשִׁיב:


**and they remained silent** They were silent and ashamed, and they did not know what to answer.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:9:1)


**לֹא טוֹב הַדָּבָר.** רַע הַדָּבָר בִּפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**The thing...is not good** The thing is evil in the eyes of the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:9:2)


**מֵחֶרְפַּת.** מִפְּנֵי חֶרְפּוֹת הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת שֶׁהֵם **אוֹיְבֵינוּ**:


**because of the reproach** because of the reproach of the heathens who are our enemies.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:10:1)


**וְגַם אֲנִי אַחַי וּנְעָרַי.** אַף **אֲנִי** וַחֲבֵרַי נוֹשִׁים בָּהֶם מָמוֹן, וַאֲנַחְנוּ נַעֲזֹב וְנִמְחֹל לָהֶם כָּל הַהַלְוָאוֹת הַלָּלוּ:


**I, too, and my brothers and my servants** I, too, and my colleagues lent them money, and we shall abandon and relinquish to them all these loans.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:10:2)


**נוֹשִׁים.** כְּמוֹ (מלכים ב ד:א) ״וְהַנֹּשֶׁה בָּא לָקַחַת״:


**have lent** Heb. נֹשִׁים, like (II Kings 4:1): “...and the creditor (וְהַנֹשֶּׁה) has come to take.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:11:1)


**הָשִׁיבוּ נָא.** וְאַף אַתֶּם תַּעֲשׂוּ כָּמוֹנִי לִמְחֹל לָהֶם כָּל חוֹבוֹתֵיהֶם:


**give back** And you shall also do as I do, to relinquish all their debts to them.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:11:2)


**וּמְאַת הַכֶּסֶף.** וְרוֹב הַכֶּסֶף שֶׁהֵם חַיָּבִים לָכֶם עִם **הַדָּגָן וְתִירוֹשׁ וְגוֹ׳**:


**and the hundred silver pieces** and the large amount of money that they owe you with the corn and the wine, etc.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:12:1)


**וַיֹּאמְרוּ נָשִׁיב.** וְהֵם אָמְרוּ לְהָשִׁיב וְלִמְחֹל הַכֹּל:


**And they said, “We shall return”** And they said [that they were willing] to return and to relinquish all.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:12:2)


**לַעֲשׂוֹת כַּדָּבָר הַזֶּה.** שֶׁיְּהוּ מוֹחֲלִים גַּם הֵם:


**to do according to this promise** that they too should relinquish.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:13:1)


**גַּם חָצְנִי נָעַרְתִּי.** אַף אֲנִי בְּגָדַי נִעַרְתִּי. חָצְנִי כְּמוֹ ״וְהֵבִיאוּ בָנַיִךְ בְּחֹצֶן״ (ישעיה מט:כב):


**I also shook out my garment** Heb. חָצְנִי, I also shook out my garment. חָצְנִי is like (Isa. 49: 22): “...and they shall bring your sons on the skirt of their garments (בְּחֹצֶן).”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:13:2)


**נָעַרְתִּי.** אֵישְׁקוֹלִיי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״הִתְנַעֲרִי מֵעָפָר״ (ישעיה נב:ב):


**my garment** is escrol(l)e in Old French, I shook out, like (ibid. 52:2): “Shake yourself (הִתְנַעֲרִי) from the dust.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:13:3)


**כָּכָה יְנַעֵר.** כָּכָה יְנַעֵר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מִן הָעוֹלָם וּמִנִּכְסֵיהֶם כָּל אוֹתָם שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ מוֹחֲלִין חוֹבוֹתֵיהֶם:


**So shall...shake out** So shall the Holy One, blessed be He, shake out from the world and from their property those who do not relinquish their debts.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:13:4)


**נָעוּר וָרֵק.** בְּלֹא נְכָסִים:


**shake out and empty** without property.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:1)


**אֲשֶׁר צִוָּה אוֹתִי.** הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**that he commanded me** i.e., the king.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:2)


**פֶּחָם.** כְּמוֹ **פֶּחָה** שָׁלְטָן, דּוּגְמַת רֵיקָם שֶׁהוּא כְּמוֹ רֵֵיקָה:


**governor** Heb. פֶּחָם, like פֶּחָה, ruler, after the pattern of רֵיקָם, empty, like רֵֵֵיקָה.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:3)


**מִשְּׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים.** לְמֶלֶךְ דָּרְיָוֶשׁ, כִּי אָז עָלָה מִבָּבֶל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (נחמיה ב:א) ״וַיְהִי בְחֹדֶשׁ נִיסָן שְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים וְגוֹ׳״:


**from the twentieth year** of King Darius, for then he ascended from Babylon, as it is stated (2:1): “Now it came to pass in the month of Nissan in the twentieth year, etc.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:4)


**שָׁנִים שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה.** שֶׁהֵם שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה:


**twelve years** which equal twelve years.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:5)


**אֲנִי וְאַחַי.** אֲנִי וַחֲבֵרַי אֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ עִמִּי:


**my brethren and I** I and my colleagues who came with me.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:14:6)


**לֶחֶם הַפֶּחָה לֹא אָכַלְתִּי.** לֶחֶם שֶׁהוּא רָאוּי לְמַאֲכַל הַפֶּחָה, שֶׁהָעָם נוֹתְנִין אוֹתוֹ אֶל הַפֶּחָה מִן הַמַּס:


**have not eaten the food allotted to the governor** the food that was fit for the governor, which the people give the governor from the tax.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:15:1)


**הִכְבִּידוּ עַל הָעָם.** הִכְבִּידוּ מַס עַל הָעָם:


**burdened the people** Heb. הִכְבִּידוּ, lit. made heavy. They made the tax heavy upon the people.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:15:2)


**וַיִּקְחוּ.** וְהָיוּ לוֹקְחִים מֵהֶם בִּשְׁבִיל הַמַּס לֶחֶם וָיַיִן אַחַר נְתִינַת אַרְבָּעִים שְׁקָלִים שֶׁל כֶּסֶף, וְכָךְ הָיוּ רְגִילִים בְּכָל שָׁנָה:


**and took** They would take bread and wine from them for the tax after they had already given forty silver shekels, and they were accustomed to doing this every year.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:15:3)


**גַּם נַעֲרֵיהֶם.** שֶׁל פַּחוֹת:


**also their servants** [i.e., the servants] of the governors.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:16:1)


**הַחוֹמָה.** חוֹמַת הָעִיר:


**wall** the wall of the city.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:16:2)


**הֶחֱזַקְתִּי.** הִתְעַסַּקְתִּי לַעֲסוֹק בָּהּ תָּדִיר:


**I adhered** I busied myself to engage in it constantly.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:16:3)


**וְשָׂדֶה לֹא קָנִינוּ.** וּבַשָּׂדוֹת שֶׁל הַיְּהוּדִים שֶׁהָיוּ בּוֹנִים הַחוֹמָה **לֹא קָנִינוּ** אֲנִי וּבְנֵי סִיעָתִי עַל עֵסֶק דָּחְקָם, שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹסְקִין בְּבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה, אֲבָל הָיִיתִי נוֹתֵן לָהֶם מָמוֹן כְּדֵי לִבְנוֹת הַבִּנְיָן:


**and we did not buy a field** But neither I nor my associates bought the fields of the Jews who were building the wall because of their poverty; they were engaged in building the wall, but I gave them money in order to build the wall.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:16:4)


**עַל הַמְּלָאכָה.** לִבְנוֹת תָּדִיר:


**over the work** to build constantly.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:17:1)


**מֵאָה וַחֲמִשִּׁים אִישׁ.** הָיוּ אוֹכְלִים עַל שֻׁלְחָנִי, לְבַד הַגֵּרִים הַבָּאִים אֵלֵינוּ מִן הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, שֶׁאַף הֵם הָיוּ אוֹכְלִים עַל שֻׁלְחָנִי:


**one hundred and fifty men** were eating at my table, in addition to the proselytes who came to us from the gentiles, for they too were eating at my table.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:1)


**וַאֲשֶׁר הָיָה נַעֲשֶׂה.** וּבְכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם הָיוּ אוֹכְלִים עַל שֻׁלְחָנִי שׁוֹר אֶחָד וְשֵׁשׁ צֹאן וְצִפֳּרִים הַרְבֵּה:


**And what was prepared** And every day they ate at my table one ox, six sheep and many birds.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:2)


**בְּרוּרוֹת.** פִּתְרוֹנוֹ לְפִי עִנְיָנוֹ: שֵׁשׁ צֹאן עֲשׂוּיוֹת, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״חָמֵשׁ צֹאן עֲשׂוּיוֹת״ (שמואל א כה:יח):


**prepared** Heb. בְּרֻרוֹת. Its interpretation is according to the context: six prepared sheep, like the matter that is stated (I Sam. 25:18): “...five prepared (עֲשׂוּיוֹת) sheep.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:3)


**נַעֲשׂוּ לִי.** לְשׁוֹן תִּקּוּן, כְּמוֹ ״וּבֶן הַבָּקָר אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה״ (בראשית יח:ח):


**were prepared for me** Heb. נַעֲשּׂוּ, an expression of preparation, like (Gen. 18:8): “...and the calf that he had prepared (עָשָּׂה).”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:4)


**וּבֵין עֲשֶׂרֶת יָמִים.** וּבְכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם הָיוּ שׁוֹתִים יַיִן הַרְבֵּה כְּדֵי שִׁיעוּר סִפּוּק שֶׁל עֲשֶׂרֶת יָמִים:


**and as much as ten days’ supply** Heb. וּבֵין עֲשֶּׂרֶת יָמִים, lit. and between ten days. And every day they drank much wine, the amount that suffices for ten days.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:5)


**וְעִם זֶה.** וּבִשְׁבִיל כָּל מַעֲשֶׂה זֶה שֶׁהָיִיתִי מוֹצִיא הוֹצָאוֹת כָּל כָּךְ:


**and for this** Heb. וְעִם זֶה ; and this procedure, for which I used to make such big expenditures.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 5:18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/5:18:6)


**לֶחֶם**. שֶׁל מַס הָרָאוּי לַפֶּחָה לֹא בִקַּשְׁתִּי וְלֹא שָׁאַלְתִּי מִן הָעָם, שֶׁהֲרֵי כָּבְדָה וְחָזָקָה עֲבוֹדַת בִּנְיַן הַחוֹמָה עֲלֵיהֶם:


**food** of the tax, which is fit for the governor, I did not seek, and I did not request it of the people, for the work of the building of the wall was heavy and strong upon them.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:1:1)


**וְלֹא נוֹתַר בָּהּ.** בְּחוֹמַת הָעִיר:


**and no breach was left therein** in the wall of the city.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:1:2)


**דְּלָתוֹת לֹא הֶעֱמַדְתִּי.** שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשׂוּ דְלָתוֹת בְּשַׁעֲרֵי הָעִיר:


**I had not erected** that doors were not made in the gates of the city.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:2:1)


**בַּכְּפִירִים.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם הוּא:


**in Kefirim** This is a name of a place.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:2:2)


**בְּבִקְעַת אוֹנוֹ.** כָּךְ שֵׁם הַבִּקְעָה:


**[which is] in the Valley of Ono** That is the name of the valley.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:2:3)


**וְהֵמָּה חוֹשְׁבִים.** הֵבַנְתִּי מֵהֶם שֶׁהָיוּ חוֹשְׁבִים עָלַי לְהָרְגֵנִי שָׁם:


**and they were plotting** against me. I understood from them that they were plotting against me to assassinate me there.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:3:1)


**מְלָאכָה גְדוֹלָה אֲנִי עֹשֶׂה.** מִבִּנְיָנֵי (הָעִיר. ספרים אחרים) הֶעָפָר:


**“I am doing great work** of the buildings (of the city); other editions read: [of the buildings] of dust.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:3:2)


**לָמָּה תִשְׁבַּת.** לָמָּה תִּהְיֶה הַמְּלָאכָה בְּטֵלָה?


**Why should...stop** Why should the work be curtailed?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:3:3)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר אַרְפֶּהָ.** יָדִי מִן הַבִּנְיָן:


**when I leave it** Heb. אַרְפֶּהָ, lit. [slacken] my hand from the building.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:3:4)


**וְיָרַדְתִּי אֲלֵיכֶם.** לְדַבֵּר וּלְהִוָּעֵד אֲלֵיכֶם:


**and come down to you** to speak with and to meet with you.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:5:1)


**אֶת נַעֲרוֹ.** עַל יְדֵי נַעֲרוֹ:


**with his servant** through his servant.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:5:2)


**וְאִגֶּרֶת פְּתוּחָה בְּיָדוֹ.** כָּתַב אִגֶּרֶת שָׁלַח לִי עַל יָדוֹ:


**and an open letter was in his hand** He sent me a written letter through him.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:6:1)


**כָּתוּב בָּהּ.** בְּאוֹתָהּ אִגֶּרֶת:


**It was written in it** in that letter.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:6:2)


**בַּגּוֹיִם נִשְׁמָע.** שֶׁנּוֹדַע לְכָל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת שֶׁאַתָּה וְכָל **הַיְּהוּדִים חוֹשְׁבִים לִמְרוֹד**:


**It is heard among the nations** that it is known to all the nations that you and all the Jews are plotting to rebel.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:6:3)


**וְגַשְׁמוּ אוֹמֵר.** גַּשְׁמוּ הוּא גֶּשֶׁם הָעֲרָבִי (נחמיה ב:יט), וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ כְּאָדָם שֶׁאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵירוֹ כָּךְ הוּא הַדָּבָר וּפְלוֹנִי אוֹמֵר:


**and Gashmu says** Gashmu is Geshem the Arab, and its interpretation is like a man who says to his friend, “This is the matter, and so-and-so says.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:6:4)


**כַּדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה.** מוּסָב עַל **כָּתוּב בָּהּ;** וּמִקְרָא מְסֹרָס הוּא, וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּאִלּוּ נֶאֱמַר: **כָּתוּב בָּהּ כַּדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה בַּגּוֹיִם נִשְׁמַע וְגוֹ׳**:


**according to these words** This refers back to the text, and it is a transposed verse. It is as though it was written, “According to these words was heard among the nations, etc.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:7:1)


**וְגַם נְבִיאִים וְגוֹ׳.** עַד **וְנִוָּעֲצָה יַחְדָּו** הָיָה כָתוּב בָּאִגֶּרֶת:


**...also...speakers** Until “...and take counsel together” was written in the letter.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:7:2)


**נְבִיאִים.** בַּעֲלֵי לָשׁוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״בּוֹרֵא נִיב שְׂפָתָיִם״ (ישעיה נז:יט):


**speakers** Heb. נְבִיאִים, speakers, like (Isa. 57:19): “I created the speech (נִיב) of the lips.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:7:3)


**לֵאמֹר.** שֶׁאוֹמְרִים: יֵשׁ מֶלֶךְ בִּיהוּדָה:


**saying** who say that there is a king in Judea.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:7:4)


**וְעַתָּה יִשָּׁמַע לַמֶּלֶךְ.** אַחֲרֵי שֶׁנִּקְרֵאתָ מֶלֶךְ יִשָּׁמַע לַמֶּלֶךְ כַּדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה:


**and now it will be reported to the king** After you have been called king, it will be reported to the king according to these words.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:8:1)


**כִּי מִלִּבְּךָ.** הֲרֵי **כִּי** בִּמְקוֹם אֶלָּא:


**from your heart** Heb. כִּי. This כִּי is in place of אֶלָּא, but.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:8:2)


**בּוֹדָם.** קוֹנְטְרַבְנְטַאלְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ אֲשֶׁר בָּדָה מִלִּבּוֹ:


**invent them** contro(u)ves eles in Old French; you are concocting them, like (I Kings 12:33): “...that he had fabricated (בָּדָה) from his heart.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:9:1)


**כִּי כֻלָּם.** כִּי כָּל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**For they all** for all the nations.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:9:2)


**מְיָרְאִים אוֹתָנוּ.** מִתְיָרְאִים מִמֶּנּוּ, וְכָךְ הֵם אוֹמְרִים: **יִרְפּוּ יְדֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳**:


**fear us** Heb. מְיָרְאִים. They fear us, and so they say, “Let their hands be slackened, etc.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:9:3)


**וְעַתָּה חַזֵּק אֶת יָדִי.** לְסַיְּעֵנִי בְּבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה:


**and now, strengthen my hands** to help me with the building of the wall.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:10:1)


**וְהוּא עָצוּר.** שֶׁמְּצָאוֹ בְּבֵיתוֹ:


**and he was confined** for he found him at home.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:10:2)


**וַיֹּאמֶר נוֹעֵד.** אוֹתוֹ שְׁמַעְיָה הָיָה נְבִיא שֶׁקֶר, וְקִבֵּל שֹׁחַד מִן סַנְבַלַּט וַחֲבֵרָיו לְהַפְחִיד אֶת נְחֶמְיָה, כְּדֵי לְבַטֵּל אֶת הַמְּלָאכָה:


**and he said, “Let us meet** Shemaiah was a false prophet who had received a bribe from Sanballat and his colleagues to frighten Nehemiah in order to stop the work.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:10:3)


**כִּי בָאִים לְהָרְגֶךָ.** שֶׁהֲרֵי יָבוֹאוּ לְהָרְגְךָ סַנְבַלַּט וַחֲבֵרָיו:


**for they are coming to assassinate you** for Sanballat and his colleagues will come to assassinate you.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:10:4)


**וְלַיְלָה בָּאִים.** וְעַל כֵּן הִזְהִירוּ לִיכָּנֵס בַּהֵיכָל וְלִסְגֹּר הַדְּלָתוֹת עָלָיו, שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא נַעֲשׂוּ דַלְתוֹת שַׁעֲרֵי הָעִיר, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּתְחִלַּת הַפָּרָשָׁה: ״גַּם עַד הָעֵת הַהִיא דְּלָתוֹת לֹא הֶעֱמַדְתִּי בַשְּׁעָרִים״ (נחמיה ו:א):


**and tonight they are coming** Therefore, he warned him to enter the Temple and to close the doors about himself, because the doors of the gates of the city had not been made, as it says at the beginning of the chapter (verse 1): “Also that until that time, I had not erected doors in the gates.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:11:1)


**הַאִישׁ כָּמוֹנִי יִבְרַח.** בִּתְמִיהָּ אִם **אִישׁ כָּמוֹנִי יִבְרַח** לִיכָּנֵס בַּהֵיכָל:


**Will a man like me flee** This is a question: Will a man like me flee to enter the Temple?


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:11:2)


**לֹא אָבוֹא.** לֹא אֶכָּנֵס שָׁם מִפְּנֵי פַּחַד הַמִּיתָה, וְלֹא אֶעֱבֹר עַל הַמִּצְוָה שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁלֹּא יָבוֹא וְיִכָּנֵס זָר בְּהֵיכָלוֹ:


**I will not enter** I will not enter there because of fear of death, and I will not transgress the commandments of the Holy One, blessed be He, that a stranger (a non-Kohen) may not enter His Temple.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:12:1)


**וָאַכִּירָה.** הִכַּרְתִּי בִּדְבָרָיו שֶׁלֹּא שְׁלָחוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּדָבָר זֶה שֶׁאֶעֱבֹר עֲבֵרָה מִפְּנֵי פַּחַד הַמִּיתָה:


**And I discerned** I discerned in his words that the Holy One, blessed be He, had not sent him on this mission, but that he wanted me to commit a transgression because of fear of death.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:12:2)


**כִּי הַנְּבוּאָה דִּבֶּר עָלַי.** שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא דִּבֵּר הַדְּבָרִים הַלָּלוּ עָלַי בְּשֵׁם נְבוּאָה:


**for he spoke prophesy concerning me** for he spoke these words concerning me in the name of prophecy.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:12:3)


**שְׂכָרוֹ.** כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד מֵהֶם **שְׂכָרוֹ**, וְנָתְנוּ לוֹ שָׂכָר לְהַפְחִידֵנִי וּלְבַטֵּל בְּבִנְיַן הַחוֹמָה:


**had hired him** Heb. שְּׂכָרוֹ. Each one of them had hired him and given him pay to frighten me and to curtail the building of the wall.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:13:1)


**לְמַעַן שָׁכוּר הוּא.** אָמַר לִי הַדְּבָרִים הַלָּלוּ:


**Because he was hired** he told me these things.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:13:2)


**לְמַעַן אִירָא.** כְּדֵי שֶׁאֶתְיָרֵא מֵהֶם לִהְיוֹת עוֹשֶׂה כֵּן לִיכָּנֵס בַּהֵיכָל וְלִהְיוֹת חוֹטֵא:


**in order that I fear** in order that I fear them, to do this to enter the Temple and sin.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:13:3)


**וְהָיָה לָהֶם לְשֵׁם רָע.** לְמַעַן שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לָהֶם **לְשֵׁם רָע**, לְחָרְפֵנִי בַּעֲבֵרָה זוֹ:


**so that they should have this for a scandal** in order that they should have this as a scandal to reproach me with this sin.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:14:1)


**וְגַם לְנוֹעַדְיָה הַנְּבִיאָה.** הִיא וּשְׁאָר נְבִיאֵי הַשֶּׁקֶר קִבְּלוּ שָׂכָר מִסַּנְבַלַּט וַחֲבֵרָיו כְּדֵי לְהַפְחִידֵנִי בִּנְבוּאַת שִׁקְרֵיהֶם:


**and also Noadiah the prophetess** She and the rest of the false prophets received pay from Sanballat and his cohorts in order to frighten me with their false prophecies.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:15:1)


**בְּעֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה.** יָמִים לְחֹדֶשׁ אֱלוּל בִּשְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ, שֶׁבִּשְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ עָלָה נְחֶמְיָה מִבָּבֶל וּבְאוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה בָּנָה חָרְבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלָיִם:


**on the twenty-fifth** day of the month of Ellul, in the twentieth year of King Darius, for in the twentieth year of Darius, Nehemiah went up from Babylon, and in that year he built the ruins of Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:15:2)


**לַחֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁנַיִם יוֹם.** סוֹף חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם יוֹם מִיּוֹם שֶׁהִתְחִילוּ לִבְנוֹתוֹ נִגְמַר בִּנְיַן הַחוֹמָה:


**after fifty-two days** The construction of the wall was completed fifty-two days after they had started building it.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:16:1)


**וַיְהִי כַּאֲשֶׁר שָׁמְעוּ כָּל אוֹיְבֵינוּ.** דָּבָר זֶה שֶׁנִּשְׁלַם בִּנְיַן הַחוֹמָה:


**Now it came to pass when all our enemies heard** this matter, that the construction of the wall was completed.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:16:2)


**וַיִּרְאוּ כָל הַגּוֹיִם.** וְכַאֲשֶׁר רָאוּ כָל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת שֶׁנִּגְמַר בִּנְיַן חוֹמַת הָעִיר:


**that all the nations became frightened** when all the nations saw that the construction of the wall of the city had been completed.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:16:3)


**וַיִּפְּלוּ מְאֹד בְּעֵינֵיהֶם.** נָפְלוּ הֵם עַצְמָם בְּעֵינֵיהֶם שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹאֲסִים בְּעַצְמָם, כִּי נָפַל פַּחַד הַיְּהוּדִים עֲלֵיהֶם:


**and they fell very low in their own eyes** They themselves fell in their own eyes; they despised themselves, for the fear of the Jews had fallen upon them.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:17:1)


**חוֹרֵי יְהוּדָה.** שָׂרֵי יְהוּדָה:


**the rulers of Judea** Heb. חֹרֵי, the princes of Judea.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:17:2)


**עַל טוֹבִיָּה.** אֶל טוֹבִיָּה. יִשְׂרָאֵל רָשָׁע הָיָה:


**to Tobiah** Heb. עַל-טוֹבִיָה. He was a renegade Israelite.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:17:3)


**וַאֲשֶׁר לְטוֹבִיָּה.** וְאִגֶּרֶת אֲשֶׁר לְטוֹבִיָּה:


**and Tobiah’s** i.e., Tobiah’s letters.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:18:1)


**כִּי רַבִּים בִּיהוּדָה.** שֶׁהָיוּ שָׂרִים רַבִּים בִּיהוּדָה אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ לוֹ לְטוֹבִיָּה בַּעֲלֵי בְּרִית וּשְׁבוּעָה:


**For many in Judea** Heb. רַבִּים, who were great rulers in Judea, were partners in a covenant and an oath with him.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:18:2)


**כִּי חָתָן הוּא.** אוֹתוֹ טוֹבִיָּה לִשְׁכַנְיָה:


**for he was the son-in-law** Tobiah [was the son-in-law] of Shechaniah.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:18:3)


**וִיהוֹחָנָן בְּנוֹ**. שֶׁל טוֹבִיָּה:


**and Jehohanan his son** [the son] of Tobiah.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:18:4)


**בַּת מְשֻׁלָּם.** אוֹתוֹ מְשֻׁלָּם שַׂר וְגָדוֹל הָיָה:


**the daughter of Meshullam** Meshullam was a great man and a ruler.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:19:1)


**גַּם טוֹבוֹתָיו.** שֶׁל טוֹבִיָּה הָיוּ מְסַפְּרִים לְפָנַי אוֹתָן בַּעֲלֵי שְׁבוּעָתוֹ וּקְרוֹבָיו:


**also of his good qualities** His confederates and his kinsmen would speak of them before me.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:19:2)


**טוֹבוֹתָיו.** שֶׁשְׁפְּרוֹאוּצְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**his good qualities** Heb. טוֹבֹתְָיו ses proezes, in Old French, his gallant exploits.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:19:3)


**הָיוּ מוֹצִיאִים לוֹ.** לְהַגִּיד לוֹ לְטוֹבִיָּה:


**they divulged to him** to tell Tobiah.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 6:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/6:19:4)


**אִגְּרוֹת שָׁלַח טוֹבִיָּה.** אִגְּרוֹת הָיָה שׁוֹלֵחַ תָּדִיר לְהַפְחִידֵנִי, שֶׁלֹּא לִבְנוֹת בִּנְיַן הַחוֹמָה:


**Tobiah sent letters** He constantly sent letters to frighten me so that we would not build the wall.



## Chapter 7


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:1:1)


**וַיִּפָּקְדוּ.** עַתָּה נַעֲשׂוּ הַלְוִיִּם פְּקוּדִים עַל פְּקִידוּתָן, לִהְיוֹתָן שׁוֹעֲרִים וּמְשׁוֹרְרִים מִן הַלְוִיִּם בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**were appointed** The Levites were now made appointees over their stations to be gatekeepers and singers in the Temple.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:2:1)


**עַל יְרוּשָׁלִָם.** לִשְׁמֹר שַׁעֲרֵי יְרוּשָׁלָיִם:


**over Jerusalem** to guard the gates of Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:2:2)


**מֵרַבִּים.** מִיָּמִים רַבִּים:


**for a long time** Heb. מֵרַבִּים, lit. from many; from many days.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:3:1)


**עַד חֹם הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ.** עַד חֲצִי הַיּוֹם, שֶׁהָיָה הַיּוֹם גָּדוֹל:


**until the sun becomes hot** until midday, for the day was long.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:3:2)


**וְעַד הֵם עוֹמְדִים.** וּבְעוֹד שֶׁהַשְּׁעָרִים פְּתוּחִים יִהְיוּ עוֹמְדִים, שֶׁלֹּא יָזוּזוּ עַד יָגִיפוּ וְיִסְגְּרוּ הַדְּלָתוֹת, וְהָרוֹצֶה לָצֵאת יֵצֵא מִן הָעִיר וְהָרוֹצֶה לִיכָּנֵס יָבֹא וְיִכָּנֵס. כָּל כָּךְ הָיוּ עוֹשִׂים מִפְּנֵי הָאוֹיְבִים:


**and they shall stand until** And as long as the doors are open, they shall stand; they shall not move until the doors are locked shut, and whoever wishes to leave may leave the city, and whoever wishes to enter may come and enter. They did all this because of the enemies.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:3:3)


**וְאֶחֱזוּ.** יֹאחֲזוּ הַדְּלָתוֹת לְסָגְרָם:


**and hold them** They should hold the doors to lock them.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:3:4)


**מִשְׁמְרוֹת יוֹשְׁבֵי יְרוּשָׁלִָם.** מִיּוֹשְׁבֵי יְרוּשָׁלִַם הֶעֱמִידוּ מִשְׁמָרוֹת, גִּיטְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**watches among the inhabitants of Jerusalem** of the inhabitants of Jerusalem station watches, jetes in Old French, (groups of) watchmen.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:4:1)


**רְחַבַת יָדַיִם.** רְחַבַת מָקוֹם וּגְדוֹלָה:


**a wide place** Heb. רַחֲבַת יָדַיִם, a wide and large place.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:5:1)


**לְהִתְיַחֵשׂ.** לַחֲקוֹר יִחוּסָן:


**to trace their lineage** Heb. לְהִתְיַחֵישׂ, to search for their lineage.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:5:2)


**הָעוֹלִים בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה.** מֵאוֹתָן הַיְּהוּדִים הָעוֹלִים עִם עֶזְרָא **בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה**:


**those who went up at first** of those Jews who went up with Ezra at first.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:5:3)


**וָאֶמְצָא כָתוּב בּוֹ.** כָּל אֵלֶּה הַדְּבָרִים הָיוּ כְתוּבִים בְּאוֹתָהּ אִגֶּרֶת:


**and I found written in it** All these words were written in that letter.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:6:1)


**אֵלֶּה בְּנֵי הַמְּדִינָה וְגוֹ׳.** עַד ״וְהַגּוֹרָלוֹת הִפַּלְנוּ עַל קָרְבַּן הָעֵצִים״ (נחמיה י:לה):


**These are the people of the province, etc.** until (10:35): “And we cast lots for the wood sacrifice.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:6:2)


**וְלִיהוּדָה.** לִמְדִינַת יְהוּדָה׃


**and to Judea** to the province of Judea.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:6:3)


**אִישׁ לְעִירוֹ.** כָּל אֶחָד לְעִירוֹ:


**each one to his city** lit. a man to his city.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:7:1)


**הַבָּאִים עִם זְרֻבָּבֶל.** אֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ מִשְּׁבִי הַגּוֹלָה עִם זְרֻבָּבֶל:


**who came with Zerubbabel** who came from the captivity of the exile with Zerubbabel.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/7:7:2)


**יֵשׁוּעַ.** הוּא יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בֶּן יְהוֹצָדָק הַכֹּהֵן הַגָּדוֹל (חגי א:א). חֶשְׁבּוֹנוֹת הַלָּלוּ פְּעָמִים הֵם מְכֻוָּנִים עִם חֶשְׁבּוֹנוֹת שֶׁל מַעְלָה, פְּעָמִים יֵשׁ שֶׁאֵינָם מְכֻוָּנִים זֶה כְּנֶגֶד זֶה; וְלֹא דִקְדֵּק הַמִּקְרָא בַּחֶשְׁבּוֹנוֹת כָּל כָּךְ, אֲבָל הַכְּלָל שָׁוֶה (עַיֵּן בְּעֶזְרָא סִימָן ב פ״ב וְצָרִיךְ עִיּוּן יסוד ושרש העבודה) לְכָאן וּלְכָאן, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״כָּל הַקָּהָל כְּאֶחָד וְגוֹ׳״ (נחמיה ז:סו). וְעַל זֶה הַכְּלָל סָמַךְ כּוֹתֵב הַסֵּפֶר, וְלֹא דִקְדֵּק בְּחֶשְׁבּוֹן הַפְּרָטִיּוֹת כָּל כָּךְ, וְהַכֹּל מְפֹרָשׁ לְמַעְלָה:


**Jeshua** This is Joshua the son of Jehozadak, the High Priest. These figures sometimes coincide with the above figures (Ezra 2); sometimes there are some that do not coincide. Scripture was not so exact with the figures, but the total is the same in both places, as it is stated (Ezra 2:64, Neh. 7:66): “The entire congregation together was forty-two thousand three hundred and sixty.” The writer relied on this total and was not so exact in the figures of the individual numbers. Everything is explained above (Ezra 2).



## Chapter 8


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:2:1)


**בְּיוֹם אֶחָד לַחֹדֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִי.** הוּא יוֹם שֶׁל רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה (ראש השנה א,א):


**on the first day of the seventh month** That is the day of Rosh Hashanah.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:3:1)


**מִן הָאוֹר.** שֶׁל תְּחִלַּת הַיּוֹם:


**from the light** of the beginning of the day.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:3:2)


**אֶל סֵפֶר הַתּוֹרָה.** הָיוּ כָל הָעָם מַטִּים אָזְנֵיהֶם:


**to the Scroll of the Law** All the people inclined their ears.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:4:1)


**אֲשֶׁר עָשׂוּ לַדָּבָר.** אוֹתוֹ מִגְדַּל עֵץ עָשׂוּ לְשֵׁם כָּךְ, לִקְרוֹת שָׁם עָלָיו סֵפֶר הַתּוֹרָה:


**on a wooden tower** That wooden tower was made for the purpose of reading the Scroll of the Law upon it.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:5:1)


**כִּי מֵעַל כָּל הָעָם הָיָה.** וְעַל כֵּן רָאוּהוּ כֻּלָּם:


**for he was above all the people** Therefore, they all saw him.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:5:2)


**וּכְפִתְחוֹ עָמְדוּ כָל הָעָם.** וְכַאֲשֶׁר פְּתָחוֹ לִקְרוֹת שָׁתְקוּ כָּל הָעָם׃


**and when he opened it, all the people stood** And when he opened it to read, all the people stood silent.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:5:3)


**עָמְדוּ.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁתִיקָה (שָׁתְקוּ), כְּמוֹ ״עָמְדוּ וְלֹא עָנוּ עוֹד״ (אִיּוֹב לב:טז):


**stood** Heb. עָמְדוּ, an expression of silence (they stood silent), like (Job 32:16): “...for they remained silent (עָמְדוּ) ; they no longer answered.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:6:1)


**בְּמֹעַל יְדֵיהֶם.** בִּנְשִׂיאוּת יְדֵיהֶם, שֶׁנָּשְׂאוּ כַפֵּיהֶם לְמַעְלָה לְהוֹדוֹת לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וַיִּפְרֹשׂ כַּפָּיו הַשָּׁמָיִם״ (מְלָכִים א ח:כב):


**with the uplifting of their hands** with the raising of their hands, meaning that they lifted their hands on high to thank the Holy One, blessed be He, as it is stated: (I Kings 8:22): “...and spread forth his hands towards heaven.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:7:1)


**מְבִינִים אֶת הָעָם.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְתַרְגְּמִין לָעָם דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה:


**explained...to the people** meaning that they would translate the words of the Torah to the people.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:7:2)


**עַל עָמְדָם.** שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹמְדִים עַל רַגְלֵיהֶם:


**in their place** meaning that they were standing on their feet.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:8:1)


**וְשׂוּם שֵׂכֶל.** וְשִׂימַת חָכְמָה, וְשׂוּם לְשׁוֹן פָּעוּל:


**and gave sense** Heb. וְשּׂם שֶׂכֶל, and giving wisdom; וְשּׂם is an expression of doing.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:9:1)


**הַיּוֹם קָדוֹשׁ.** כִּי יוֹם רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה הוּא:


**The day is holy** because it is the day of Rosh Hashanah.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:9:2)


**כִּי בוֹכִים כָּל הָעָם.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁלֹּא קִיְּמוּ הַתּוֹרָה כָּרָאוּי:


**for all the people were weeping** because they did not uphold the Torah appropriately.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:10:1)


**לְאֵין נָכוֹן לוֹ.** לֶעָנִי שֶׁאֵין מְזֻמָּן לוֹ מַאֲכָלוֹ:


**to whoever has nothing prepared** to a poor man who has no food prepared for himself.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:11:1)


**מְחַשִּׁים.** הָיוּ מַזְהִירִין לְכָל הָעָם לַחֲשׁוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא יִבְכּוּ עוֹד:


**quieted** They admonished all the people to be quiet, that they should weep no longer.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:11:2)


**הַסּוּ.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁתִיקָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּהַס כָּלֵב״ (במדבר יג:ל):


**Hush** Heb. הַסוּ, an expression of silence, like (Num. 13:30): “And Caleb hushed (וַיַהַס).”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:13:1)


**וּבַיּוֹם הַשֵּׁנִי.** שֶׁל רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה:


**And on the second day** of Rosh Hashanah.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:15:1)


**וַאֲשֶׁר יַשְׁמִיעוּ.** וְצִוּוּ **אֲשֶׁר יַשְׁמִיעוּ קוֹל** לָחוֹג אֶת חַג הַסֻּכּוֹת, וְכֵן נוֹהֵג הַמִּקְרָא לְדַבֵּר כָּעִנְיָן הַזֶּה, כְּמוֹ ״אֱמֹר לַנַּעַר וְיַעֲבֹר לְפָנֵינוּ״ (שמואל א ט:כז):


**And that they should announce** And they commanded that they announce that they celebrate the Festival of Sukkoth, and so it is customary for Scripture to speak in this manner, like (I Sam. 9: 27): “Tell the servant and he will go ahead of us.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:15:2)


**וַעֲלֵי הֲדַס.** מְפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת סֻכָּה (סוכה יב,א): זֶה הֲדַס שׁוֹטֶה, שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לְלוּלָב כִּי אִם לַעֲשׂוֹת סֻכָּה:


**myrtle leaves** It is explained in Tractate Succah (12a) that this is a wild myrtle which is unfit for the lulav and only fit to make a sukkah.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:15:3)


**וַעֲלֵי תְמָרִים.** לְלוּלָב:


**date palm leaves** for a lulav.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:15:4)


**וַעֲלֵי עֵץ עָבֹת.** זֶה הֲדַס הָרָאוּי לְלוּלָב, כַּמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת סֻכָּה (סוכה לב,ב):


**and leaves of plaited trees** This is the myrtle that is fit for the lulav as is explained in Tractate Succah (ad loc.).



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 8:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/8:18:1)


**יוֹם בְּיוֹם.** בְּכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם:


**day by day** every day.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:1:1)


**וַאֲדָמָה עֲלֵיהֶם.** הֶעֱלוּ עָפָר עַל רֹאשָׁם:


**and [with] earth upon them** They had put earth on their heads.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:2:1)


**מִכָּל בְּנֵי נֵכָר.** וּמִנְּשֵׁיהֶם שֶׁל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְמַעְלָה (נחמיה יג):


**from all the foreigners** and from their wives of the heathen nations, as is stated above (Ezra 10).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:4:1)


**עַל מַעֲלֵה הַלְוִיִּם.** מַעֲלוֹת הָיוּ שָׁם שֶׁהַלְוִיִּם עוֹמְדִים שָׁם עֲלֵיהֶם בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁאָמְרוּ שִׁירָה:


**on the step of the Levites** There were steps upon which the Levites would stand when they would recite their song [for the sacrificial service].



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:5:1)


**מִן הָעוֹלָם וְעַד הָעוֹלָם.** מִתְּחִלַּת הָעוֹלָם וְעַד תַּכְלִיתוֹ יְהִי בָּרוּךְ:


**from everlasting to ever lasting** From the beginning of the world until its end, He shall be blessed.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:5:2)


**וִיבָרְכוּ שֵׁם כְּבוֹדֶךָ.** עַכְשָׁיו דִּבְּרוּ הַלְוִיִּם כְּלַפֵּי שְׁכִינָה בִּתְפִלָּה: **וִיבָרְכוּ שֵׁם כְּבוֹדֶךָ** כִּי הוּא גָּדוֹל וּמְרוֹמָם עַל כָּל בְּרָכָה וּתְהִלָּה, כִּי כָל הַבְּרָכוֹת וּתְהִלּוֹת שֶׁלּוֹ הֵם. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **וּמְרוֹמָם עַל כָּל בְּרָכָה וּתְהִלָּה** שֶׁאֵין בְּרִיָּה יְכוֹלָה לְבָרְכוֹ וּלְהַלְּלוֹ כְּפִי גָדְלוֹ וְרוֹמְמוּתוֹ, שֶׁהוּא מְרוֹמָם וְנִשָּׂא יוֹתֵר מַה שֶּׁאֵין הַפֶּה יְכוֹלָה לְדַבֵּר:


**and they shall bless Your glorious Name** Now the Levites spoke toward the Shechinah in prayer, for He is great and exalted over every blessing and praise, for all the blessings and praises are His. Another explanation is that He is exalted above every blessing and praise; that no creature can bless Him or praise Him according to His greatness and His exaltedness, for He is exalted and raised higher than the mouth can express.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:7:1)


**וְשַׂמְתָּ.** טַעֲמוֹ לְמַעְלָה בַּשִּׂי״ן, לְפִי שֶׁמַּשְׁמָעוּתוֹ בִּלְשׁוֹן עָבָר:


**and made** Heb. וְשַּׂמְתָּ. The accent is on the first syllable on the “sin,” because its meaning is in the past tense.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:8:1)


**וְכָרוֹת עִמּוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן פָּעוּל:


**and You made** Heb. וְכָרוֹת, lit. and cutting, an expression of doing.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:10:1)


**כִּי הֵזִידוּ.** כִּי הִרְשִׁיעוּ עֲלֵיהֶם, כְּמוֹ ״כִּי בַדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר זָדוּ עֲלֵיהֶם״ (שמות יח:יא):


**that they dealt wickedly** Heb. הֵזִידוּ, that they dealt wickedly, like (Exod. 18:11): “...that they plotted (זָדוּ) against them.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:10:2)


**שֵׁם.** רָצוֹן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a name** Heb. שֵּׁם, renom in French, renown.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:12:1)


**יוֹמָם.** בְּכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם:


**by day** every day.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:13:1)


**וְדַבֵּר.** כְּמוֹ וּלְדַבֵּר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם הִשְׁמִיעֲךָ אֶת קוֹלוֹ לְיַסְּרֶךָּ״ (דברים ד:לו):


**and spoke** Heb. וְדַּבֵּר, like וּלְדַבֵּר, and to speak, as it says (Deut. 4:36): “From the heavens He let you hear His voice to chastise you.”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:15:1)


**לְרַעֲבָם.** מִן רָעָב יֹאמַר **לְרַעֲבָם**, כְּמוֹ מִן צָמָא **לִצְמָאָם**:


**for their hunger** Heb. לִרְעָבָם. From רָעָב one says לִרְעָבָם, as from צָמָא [one says] לִצְמָאָם.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:15:2)


**אֲשֶׁר נָשָׂאתָ אֶת יָדְךָ.** בִּשְׁבוּעָה, כְּמוֹ ״אֲשֶׁר נָשָׂאתִי אֶת יָדִי לָתֵת אֹתָהּ״ (שמות ו:ח):


**that You raised Your hand** with an oath, like (Exod. 6:8): “...which I raised My hand to give it.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:17:1)


**בְּמִרְיָם.** בִּשְׁבִיל מְרִי שֶׁלָּהֶם:


**because of their rebelliousness** Heb. בְּמִרְיָם.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:19:1)


**בְּהַדֶּרֶךְ.** כְּמוֹ בַּדֶּרֶךְ׃


**on the way** Heb. בְּהַדֶּרֶךְ, like בַּדֶּרֶךְ.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:22:1)


**לְפֵאָה.** כְּמוֹ לִהְיוֹת לְצַד זָוִית אַחַת מֻחְלָקִים, בְּלִי לְהִתְעָרֵב עִם גּוֹיֵי הָאֲרָצוֹת:


**into a corner** Heb. לְפֵאָה, like “to be to the side of one corner,” separate, not mingling with the nations of the lands.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:24:1)


**וַתִּכָּנַע.** וְהִכְנַעְתָּ:


**and You vanquished** [as translated,] and You vanquished.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:27:1)


**וְאַתָּה מִשָּׁמַיִם תִּשְׁמַע.** הָיִיתָ שׁוֹמֵעַ:


**And You from the heavens You heard** you listened.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:30:1)


**וַתִּמְשֹׁךְ עֲלֵיהֶם.** מָשַׁכְתָּ עֲלֵיהֶם רַחֲמִים, שֶׁלֹּא לְהַשְׁחִיתָם:


**But You extended upon them** You extended mercy upon them not to destroy them.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:33:1)


**עַל כָּל הַבָּא.** עַל כָּל מַעֲשֵׂה יִסּוּרֶיךָ הָרָעִים אֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ עָלֵינוּ:


**with all that has come upon us** with the entire episode of the harsh tortures that have come upon us.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:36:1)


**הִנֵּה אֲנַחְנוּ עֲבָדִים עָלֶיהָ.** שֶׁאֲנַחְנוּ מַעֲלִים מַס לְכָל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**Behold, today we are slaves** upon it [the land], for we pay tribute to all the nations.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:37:1)


**וּתְבוּאָתָהּ.** שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And its produce** [the produce] of the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:37:2)


**מַרְבֶּה.** מַעֲדַנֵּי מַאֲכָלִים לְמַלְכֵי הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**it lavishes** delicacies to the kings of the nations...


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 9:37:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:37:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/9:37:3)


**אֲשֶׁר נָתַתָּה עָלֵינוּ.** שֶׁהֵם מוֹשְׁלִים בָּנוּ:


**whom You have appointed over us** who rule over us.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:1:1)


**וּבְכָל זֹאת.** וּבְכָל הָרָעָה הַזֹּאת הַבָּאָה עָלֵינוּ אֲנַחְנוּ כּוֹרְתִים אֲמָנָה לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, לְהַאֲמִין וְלִבְטוֹחַ בּוֹ:


**Yet, despite all this** Despite all the evil that has come upon us, we are making a trust with the Holy One, blessed be He, to believe and to trust in Him.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:1:2)


**וְכוֹתְבִים.** וַאֲנַחְנוּ כּוֹתְבִים בַּסֵּפֶר לְקַבֵּל מַלְכוּתוֹ עָלֵינוּ, לְמַעַן תִּהְיֶה לָנוּ לְעֵדוּת:


**and writing** And we are writing on a scroll to accept His kingdom upon us in order that it be to us as a testimony.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:1:3)


**וְעַל הֶחָתוּם.** שֶׁרוֹב גְּדוֹלִים וַחֲשׁוּבִים שֶׁבָּנוּ חֲתוּמִים עַל סֵפֶר קַבָּלַת הַמִּצְוֹת לִהְיוֹת לָנוּ לְעֵדוּת:


**and signed thereon** for most of the great and prominent men among us have signed the scroll of the acceptance of the commandments, to be for us as a testimony.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:2:1)


**וְעַל הַחֲתוּמִים.** עַתָּה מוֹנֶה וְהוֹלֵךְ הַחֲתוּמִים בַּסֵּפֶר לְעֵדוּת קִבּוּל הַמִּצְוֹת:


**And signed thereon** He now proceeds to enumerate the signatories on the testimonial document of the acceptance of the commandments.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:2:2)


**נְחֶמְיָה הַתִּרְשָׁתָא.** שֵׁם אֶחָד הוּא, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי (עזרא ב:סג) דְּ**הַתִּרְשָׁתָא** הוּא **נְחֶמְיָה**. וְלָמָּה נִקְרָא שְׁמוֹ **הַתִּרְשָׁתָא**? שֶׁהִתִּירוּ לוֹ חֲכָמִים לִשְׁתּוֹת יֵין נֶסֶךְ, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה מַשְׁקֶה לַמֶּלֶךְ:


**Nehemiah Hattirshatha** It is one name, and this is what I explained (Ezra 2:63) that [the name] Hattirshatha refers to Nehemiah. And why was he called Hattirshatha? Because the Sages permitted him to drink wine of gentiles, because he was the butler of the king.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:29:1)


**וְכָל הַנִּבְדָּל.** הֵם הַגֵּרִים שֶׁנִּבְדְּלוּ מִתּוֹרַת הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, לִהְיוֹת דְּבוּקִים וּמְחֻבָּרִים אֶל תּוֹרָתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְלִשְׁמֹר מִצְוֹתָיו:


**and all who separated themselves** These are the proselytes who separated themselves from the practice of the nations to cling and attach themselves to the Law of the Holy One, blessed be He, and to keep His commandments.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:30:1)


**עַל אֲחֵיהֶם.** עִם אֲחֵיהֶם:


**with their brethen** Heb. עַל אֲחֵיהֶם with their brothers.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:30:2)


**אַדִּירֵיהֶם.** גְּדוֹלִים שֶׁבָּהֶם:


**their noblemen** the great ones among them.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:1)


**הַמִּקָּחוֹת.** הַסְּחוֹרוֹת:


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:2)


**שֶׁבֶר.** תְּבוּאָה, כְּמוֹ ״בַּשֶּׁבֶר אֲשֶׁר הֵם שֹׁבְרִים״ (בראשית מז:יד):


**and all grains** Heb. שֶּׁבֶר, grain, like (Gen. 47:14): “... for the grain (בַּשֶּׁבֶר) that they were purchasing.”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:3)


**וּבְיוֹם קֹדֶשׁ.** שֶׁל יוֹם טוֹב:


**or on holy days** This refers to festivals.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:4)


**וְנִטֹּשׁ אֶת הַשָּׁנָה הַשְּׁבִיעִית.** כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וְהַשְּׁבִיעִת תִּשְׁמְטֶנָּה וּנְטַשְׁתָּהּ״ (שמות כג:יא):


**and we shall abandon during the seventh year** As is stated (Exod. 23:11): “But throughout the seventh year you shall let it go and abandon it (וּנְטַשְּׁתָּה).”


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:32:5)


**וּמַשָּׂא כָל יָד.** הַשְׁמָטַת מִלְוֶה בַּשְּׁבִיעִית (דברים טו:ב):


**and the loan given by every hand** This refers to the release of loans during the seventh year.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:34:1)


**וְכָל מְלֶאכֶת בֵּית אֱלֹהֵינוּ.** לַעֲשׂוֹת מִן הַכֶּסֶף:


**and all the work of the Temple of our God** to do with money.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:35:1)


**הִפַּלְנוּ.** כַּמָּה יָבִיא כָּל אֶחָד:


**we cast** how much each one should bring.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:35:2)


**קָרְבַּן הָעֵצִים.** תִּקְרֹבֶת שֶׁל עֲצֵי הַמַּעֲרָכָה לַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


**the wood offering** an offering of wood for the arrangement on the altar.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:35:3)


**לְבֵית אֲבוֹתֵינוּ.** שֶׁהִיא בֵּית קְדֻשַּׁת אֲבוֹתֵינוּ:


**to the House of our forefathers** which is the House of the sanctity of our forefathers.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:36:1)


**וּבִכּוּרֵי כָּל פְּרִי כָּל עֵץ.** בִּכּוּרֵי שֶׁל **כָּל פְּרִי** הָאִילָן הֵם מִדִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים:


**the first fruits of our soil** The first of every fruit of a tree. They are required by rabbinical injunction.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:38:1)


**נָבִיא לַכֹּהֲנִים אֶל לִשְׁכוֹת.** כִּי שָׁם הָיוּ רְגִילִים:


**we shall bring to the priests to the chambers** for they were usually there.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:38:2)


**הַמְעַשְּׂרִים בְּכָל עָרֵי עֲבוֹדָתֵנוּ.** שֶׁנּוֹטְלִים מַעַשְׂרוֹתֵיהֶם **בְּכָל עָרֵי** יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**who receive tithes in all the cities of our work** who take their tithes in all the cities of Israel.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:39:1)


**בַּעְשֵׂר.** לְשׁוֹן בַּעְשׂוֹר:


**at the [Levites’] tithing** Heb. בַּעְשֵּׂר ;an expression meaning at the tithing.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 10:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/10:39:2)


**אֶת מַעֲשַׂר הַמַּעֲשֵׂר.** שֶׁהָיוּ הַלְוִיִּם מְעַשְּׂרִים מַעֲשֵׂר מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר וּמְבִיאִים אוֹתוֹ לַכֹּהֲנִים שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ עוֹזְבִים אֶת בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**the tithe of the tithe** for the Levites would take a tithe from the tithe and bring it up to the priests, who did not leave the Temple.



## Chapter 11


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:1:1)


**אֶחָד מִן הָעֲשָׂרָה.** שֶׁבְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, לְמַעַן יֵשְׁבוּ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם לִשְׁמֹר הָעִיר:


**one of ten** of Israel, so that they should dwell in Jerusalem to guard the city.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:1:2)


**וְתֵשַׁע הַיָּדוֹת.** תִּשְׁעָה חֲלָקִים שֶׁבְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and nine parts** nine parts of Israel.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:2:1)


**הַמִּתְנַדְּבִים.** אֲשֶׁר נָדְבָה רוּחָם אוֹתָם לִהְיוֹת יוֹשְׁבִים בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם לִשְׁמֹר הָעִיר מִן הָאוֹיְבִים:


**who volunteered** whose spirit inspired them to volunteer to dwell in Jerusalem to guard the city from enemies.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:3:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה רָאשֵׁי הַמְּדִינָה אֲשֶׁר יָשְׁבוּ בִּירוּשָׁלִָם.** הוּא שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסָמוּךְ **וּבִירוּשָׁלִַם יָשְׁבוּ בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה וְגוֹ׳**:


**And these are the heads of the province who dwelt in Jerusalem** these are the ones regarding whom it is stated below: “And in Jerusalem there dwelt of the children of Judah, etc.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:4:1)


**מִבְּנֵי יְהוּדָה עֲתָיָה וְגוֹ׳.** חוֹזֵר וּמְפָרֵשׁ **מִבְּנֵי יְהוּדָה** מִי וָמִי הָיוּ. וְכָל הַפָּרָשָׁה אֵינָהּ מוֹנָה כִּי אִם הַגְּדוֹלִים וְהַחֲשׁוּבִים שֶׁבָּהֶם:


**of the children of Judah: Athaiah, etc.** He returns to explain who those ‘of the children of Judah’ were, and the entire chapter enumerates only the great and esteemed ones among them.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:9:1)


**פָּקִיד.** מְמֻנֶּה:


**the official** the one appointed.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:9:2)


**מִשְׁנֶה.** אַחַר (הַפָּקִיד):


**the second in command** after [the official].



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:11:1)


**נָגִיד.** מְמֻנֶּה:


**the official** [as translated,] the official.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:11:2)


**נְגִיד בֵּית הָאֱלֹהִים.** מְמֻנֶּה עַל בֵּית אֱלֹהִים:


**the official over the Temple of God** appointed over the Temple of God.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:12:1)


**וַאֲחֵיהֶם עֹשֵׂה הַמְּלָאכָה לַבָּיִת.** וְחַבְרֵיהֶם שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹבְדִים עֲבוֹדַת הַבַּיִת וְקָרְבְּנוֹתָיו:


**And their brethren who performed the work of the Temple** and their colleagues, who were performing the service of the Temple and its sacrifices.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:13:1)


**רָאשִׁים לְאָבוֹת.** רָאשֵׁי בֵית אָב מִמִּשְׁפְּחוֹת הַכֹּהֲנִים:


**rulers over the fathers’ houses** the heads of the fathers’ houses of the priestly families.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:14:1)


**וּפָקִיד עֲלֵיהֶם.** מְמֻנֶּה עֲלֵיהֶם:


**and the official over them** the one appointed over them.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:16:1)


**עַל הַמְּלָאכָה הַחִיצוֹנָה.** אֲשֶׁר לַבַּיִת, כְּגוֹן בִּנְיָנֵי בֶדֶק הַבַּיִת וַעֲצֵי הַמַּעֲרָכָה שֶׁהָיוּ מְבִיאִים מִבַּחוּץ:


**over the outside work** of the Temple, such as the buildings of the repair of the Temple (sic) and the wood of the arrangement, which they would bring from outside.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:17:1)


**רֹאשׁ הַתְּחִלָּה יְהוֹדֶה לַתְּפִלָּה.** הוּא הָיָה **רֹאשׁ** וּמְמֻנֶּה וְגָדוֹל לִפְתֹּחַ תְּחִלָּה לְהוֹדוֹת **בַּתְּפִלָּה** וּבִזְמִירוֹת שִׁירוֹתֵיהֶם שֶׁל לְוִיִּם. **יְהוֹדֶה** כְּמוֹ יוֹדֶה, וְהַהֵ״א נִכְנֶסֶת בַּתֵּיבָה שֶׁלֹּא לְצֹרֶךְ, כְּמוֹ יוֹסֵף יְהוֹסֵף (תהלים פא:ו):


**the head to begin the thanksgiving in prayer** He was the head and the appointed official, and the greatest one who was first to start and to thank in prayer and in singing the songs of the Levites. יְהוֹדֶה is like יוֹדֶה, and the “hey” enters the word unnecessarily, like יוֹסֵף and יְהוֹסֵף.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:19:1)


**וְהַשּׁוֹעֲרִים.** מְמֻנִּין עַל שַׁעֲרֵי הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**And the gatekeepers** those appointed over the gates of the Temple.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:20:1)


**אִישׁ בְּנַחֲלָתוֹ.** כָּל אֶחָד בְּנַחֲלָתוֹ הָיוּ יוֹשְׁבִים׃


**each one in his inheritance** They were dwelling each one in his inheritance.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:21:1)


**בָּעֹפֶל.** מָקוֹם אֶחָד אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**in the Ophel** a place that was in Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:21:2)


**עַל הַנְּתִינִים.** מְמֻנִּים **עַל הַנְּתִינִים**:


**over the Nethinites** appointed over the Nethinites.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:22:1)


**וּפְקִיד הַלְוִיִּם.** מְמֻנֶּה עַל הַלְוִיִּם אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלִַם:


**And the official over the Levites** appointed over the Levites in Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:22:2)


**לְנֶגֶד מְלֶאכֶת.** עַל מְלֶאכֶת הַבַּיִת מְמֻנֶּה, כְּגוֹן בִּנְיָנֵי בֶּדֶק הַבַּיִת וּשְׁאָר דְּבָרִים שֶׁמּוֹצִיאִין מָמוֹן לְמַעֲשֵׂה (לְמַעֲשַׂר) הַבַּיִת, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְמַעְלָה ״וְשַׁבְּתַי וְיוֹזָבָד עַל הַמְּלָאכָה הַחִיצוֹנָה וְגוֹ׳״ (נחמיה יא:טז)׃


**over the work of the Temple of God** He was appointed over the work of the Temple, such as the buildings of the repair of the Temple and the rest of the matters for which money is spent for the work of the Temple, like the matter that is stated above (verse 16): “...and Shabbethai and Jazabad over the outside work, etc.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:23:1)


**כִּי מִצְוַת הַמֶּלֶךְ עֲלֵיהֶם.** וְהַמֶּלֶךְ הָיָה מַאֲמִין בָּהֶם:


**For the command of the king was upon them and [his] trust** The king trusted them.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:23:2)


**עַל הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים.** לְהוֹצִיא מָמוֹן מֵאוֹצַר הַמֶּלֶךְ דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**upon the singers** to spend money from King Darius’s treasury.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:23:3)


**דָּבָר יוֹם בְּיוֹמוֹ.** כְּדֵי לַעֲשׂוֹת צָרְכֵי הַבַּיִת:


**for the daily requirements** in order to perform the requirements of the Temple.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:24:1)


**לְיַד הַמֶּלֶךְ.** הוּא הָיָה מִצְרָן לַעֲצַת הַמֶּלֶךְ דָּרְיָוֶשׁ:


**at the king’s hand** He was privy to King Darius’s counsel.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:24:2)


**לְכָל דָּבָר.** לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶם אֲשֶׁר לָעָם לְדַבֵּר עֲלֵיהֶם אֶל הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**for every matter** for every matter that concerned the people, to speak to the king on their behalf.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 11:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/11:36:1)


**מַחְלְקוֹת יְהוּדָה לְבִנְיָמִין.** הָיוּ מְחַלְּקִין בְּמַחְלְקוֹתָם אָנָה וָאָנָה בְּאֶרֶץ יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין:


**there were divisions to Judah and to Benjamin** They were divided into their divisions here and there in the land of Judah and Benjamin.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:1:1)


**וְיֵשׁוּעַ.** הוּא יְהוֹשֻׁעַ הַכֹּהֵן הַגָּדוֹל:


**and Jeshua** That is Joshua the High Priest.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:7:1)


**בִּימֵי יֵשׁוּעַ.** שֶׁהָיוּ רָאשִׁים בִּימֵי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל:


**in the days of Jeshua** i.e., those who were leaders in the days of Joshua the High Priest.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:8:1)


**הֻיְּדוֹת.** מִן כְּלֵי שִׁיר, לוֹמַר שִׁירָה:


**huyedoth** [This was] a type of musical instrument which accompanied the songs.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:9:1)


**לְמִשְׁמָרוֹת.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁמָּצִינוּ (דברי הימים א כד:א ואילך) שֶׁהַלְוִיִּם הָיוּ חֲלוּקִים לְעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה מִשְׁמָרוֹת:


**in the watches** as we find (in I Chronicles from ch. 24 on) that the Levites were divided into twenty- four watches.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:10:1)


**וְיֵשׁוּעַ הוֹלִיד אֶת יוֹיָקִים וְגוֹ׳.** כָּל אֵלּוּ שֶׁבְּמִקְרָא זֶה הָיוּ כֹּהֲנִים גְּדוֹלִים:


**Now Jeshua begot Joiakim, etc.** All those [listed] in this verse were high priests.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:12:1)


**וּבִימֵי יוֹיָקִים.** שֶׁהָיָה כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל הָיוּ כֹהֲנִים הַלָּלוּ, הֵם הָיוּ רָאשִׁים, **רָאשֵׁי** בֵּית אָב לְמִשְׁמְרוֹתָם:


**And in the days of Joiakim** who was the high priest. These priests were the leaders of the fathers’ houses of their watches.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:12:2)


**לִשְׂרָיָה מְרָיָה.** לְמִשְׁמֶרֶת שֶׁל שְׂרָיָה הָיָה מְרָיָה רֹאשׁ בֵּית אָב, וּלְמִשְׁמֶרֶת שֶׁל יִרְמְיָה הָיָה חֲנַנְיָה רֹאשׁ בֵּית אָב, וְכֵן כֻּלָּם. וְאֶת אֲשֶׁר לֹא פֵּרַשׁ כָּל הָעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע מִשְׁמָרוֹת, לֹא דִקְדֵּק הַמִּקְרָא בְּכָךְ לִמְנוֹתָם כֻּלָּם:


**of Seraiah was Meraiah** Of the watch of Seraiah, the head of the father’s house was Meraiah, and of the watch of Jeremiah, the head of the father’s house was Hananiah, and so it is with them all, and the reason Scripture did not list all twenty-four watches is that it is not exacting in enumerating them all.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:22:1)


**הַלְוִיִּם בִּימֵי אֶלְיָשִׁיב.** הַלְוִיִּם הַלָּלוּ הָיוּ רָאשֵׁי בֵית אָבוֹת בְּמִשְׁמְרוֹת הַלְוִיִּם בִּימֵי אֶלְיָשִׁיב הַכֹּהֵן הַגָּדוֹל, וְהַכֹּהֲנִים הַמְּנוּיִין לְמַעְלָה בְּסָמוּךְ (פסוקים א-כא) הֵם הָיוּ רָאשֵׁי בֵית אָבוֹת בְּמִשְׁמְרוֹתָם גַּם בִּימֵי אֶלְיָשִׁיב:


**The Levites in the days of Eliashib** These Levites were the heads of the fathers’ houses in the watches of the Levites in the days of Eliashib the High Priest, and the priests enumerated immediately above were the heads of the fathers’ houses in their watches, also in the days of Eliashib.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:22:2)


**עַל מַלְכוּת.** בִּימֵי מַלְכוּת דָּרְיָוֶשׁ הָיָה דָּבָר זֶה:


**in the reign of** This matter came about in the days of Darius.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:23:1)


**וְעַד יְמֵי יוֹחָנָן.** כָּל אוֹתָן שֶׁהָיוּ רָאשֵׁי אָבוֹת הַלְוִיִּם **עַד יְמֵי יוֹחָנָן** כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל מְנוּיִין שָׁם בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים:


**even until the days of Johanan** All those who were the heads of the fathers’ houses of the Levites until the days of Johanan the High Priest were enumerated in the chronicles.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:24:1
