[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:24:1)


**מִשְׁמָר לְעֻמַּת מִשְׁמָר.** שֶׁהָיוּ חֲלוּקִים בְּמִשְׁמְרוֹתָם לוֹמַר שִׁירָה:


**a watch opposite a watch** for they were divided into their watches to recite the song.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:25:1)


**שׁוֹעֲרִים מִשְׁמָר.** שׁוֹעֲרִים הָיוּ בְמִשְׁמָר שֶׁלָּהֶם, לִשְׁמֹר שַׁעֲרֵי הַבַּיִת:


**sentries of the gates [in their] watch** The sentries of the gates were in their watch to guard the gates of the Temple.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:25:2)


**בָּאֲסוּפֵּי.** כְּמוֹ בְּסִפֵּי הַשְּׁעָרִים (דברי הימים א ט:כב):


**at the thresholds** Heb. בַּאֲסֻפֵּי, like בְּסִיפֵּי הַשְּׁעָרִים, at the thresholds of the gates.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:26:1)


**אֵלֶּה בִּימֵי יוֹיָקִים.** אֵלֶּה הָיוּ שׁוֹעֲרִים בִּימֵי יוֹיָקִים:


**These were in the days of Joiakim** These were the gate sentries in the days of Joiakim.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:27:1)


**וּבַחֲנֻכַּת חוֹמַת יְרוּשָׁלִָם.** בְּתַשְׁלוּם חוֹמַת הָעִיר, לְקַדֵּשׁ אֶת הָעִיר:


**And in the dedication of the wall of Jerusalem** at the completion of the wall of the city, to sanctify the city.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:27:2)


**וּבְתוֹדוֹת.** וּבְהוֹדָאוֹת:


**and with thanksgivings** Heb. וּבְתוֹדוֹת, and with thanksgivings.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:28:1)


**וּמִן הַכִּכָּר.** אֲשֶׁר סְבִיבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלִַם בָּאוּ הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים:


**and from the plain** the singers came from [the plain] surrounding Jerusalem.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:30:1)


**וַיִּטַּהֲרוּ.** תְּחִלָּה טִהֲרוּ אֶת עַצְמָן, וְאַחַר כָּךְ טִהֲרוּ אֶת אֲחֵרִים:


**purified themselves** First they purified themselves, and afterwards they purified the others.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:30:2)


**וְאֶת הַשְּׁעָרִים וְאֶת הַחוֹמָה.** שֶׁלֹּא תִהְיֶה טֻמְאָה שָׁם בָּעִיר:


**and the gates and the wall** that there should be no uncleanliness in the city.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:31:1)


**מֵעַל לַחוֹמָה.** שֶׁקִּדֵּשׁ אֶת הָעִיר, וְהָלְכוּ הַשָּׂרִים וּבֵית דִּין וְהַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם עִם שְׁתֵּי תוֹדוֹת סָבִיב לָעִיר. הַכֹּל כַּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת שְׁבוּעוֹת (שבועות טו,א), כְּדֵי לְקַדֵּשׁ אֶת הָעִיר:


**upon the wall** meaning that he sanctified the city, the princes, the tribunal, and the Levites with two thanksgiving offerings around the city, as is delineated in Tractate Shebuoth (14a, 15a, b), in order to sanctify the city.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:31:2)


**שְׁתֵּי תוֹדוֹת.** שְׁתֵּי לַחְמֵי תוֹדָה, כָּל לֶחֶם וָלֶחֶם קָרוּי תּוֹדָה:


**two...thanksgiving offerings** two breads of the thanksgiving offerings, each bread being called a thanksgiving offering.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:31:3)


**וְתַהֲלוּכוֹת לַיָּמִין.** וְכַאֲשֶׁר יָצְאוּ מִן הָעִיר כְּדֵי לְסוֹבְבָהּ, הָלְכוּ לְצַד יְמִינָהּ:


**with processions to the right** And when they went out of the city in order to encompass it, they went to the right side.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:34:1)


**יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין.** שְׁנֵי שָׂרִים הָיוּ:


**Judah and Benjamin** They were two princes.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:36:1)


**וְאֶחָיו.** חֲבֵירָיו שֶׁל זְכַרְיָה הַכֹּהֵן (נחמיה יב:לה):


**And his brethren** the colleagues of Zechariah the priest.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:37:1)


**וְעַל שַׁעַר הָעָיִן.** לִפְנֵי שַׁעַר הָעַיִן הָלְכוּ סְבִיבוֹת הָעִיר:


**And by the Fountain Gate** Before the Fountain Gate, they marched around the city.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:37:2)


**וְנֶגְדָּם עָלוּ.** וּלְפָנֵיהֶם עָלוּ מִ**שַּׁעַר הָעַיִן** **לְמַעֲלוֹת** שֶׁל **עִיר דָּוִד** וְאוֹתָן **מַעֲלוֹת** הָיוּ **לַחוֹמָה**:


**and opposite them** And before them they ascended from the Fountain Gate to the steps of the City of David, and those steps led to the wall.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:38:1)


**וְהַתּוֹדָה הַשֵּׁנִית הַהוֹלֶכֶת לְמוֹל.** תּוֹדָה אַחֶרֶת שֶׁהָיְתָה **שֵׁנִית** לַחֲבֶרְתָּהּ הָיְתָה **הוֹלֶכֶת** נֶגֶד חֲבֶרְתָּהּ בְּשָׁוֶה, וְלֹא זוֹ אַחַר זוֹ:


**And the second thanksgiving offering, which went opposite [it]** The other thanksgiving offering, which was second to its fellow, went opposite its fellow, alongside it, not one following the other.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:38:2)


**וַאֲנִי אַחֲרֶיהָ.** וַאֲנִי הָיִיתִי הוֹלֵךְ **אַחֲרֶיהָ** שֶׁל תּוֹדָה:


**and I was after it** And I marched after it, [i.e.,] after the thanksgiving offering.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:39:1)


**וְעָמְדוּ.** שָׁם הָיוּ עוֹמְדִים, שֶׁלֹּא לֵילֵךְ יוֹתֵר:


**and they stood** They stood, there and did not go any further.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:40:1)


**שְׁתֵּי הַתּוֹדוֹת.** שֶׁנָּשְׂאוּ אוֹתָם לְבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**the two thanksgiving offerings** that they carried to the Temple.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:41:1)


**בַּחֲצוֹצְרוֹת.** הָיוּ תּוֹקְעִין מִתּוֹךְ שִׂמְחָתָן:


**with the trumpets** They sounded the trumpets out of their joy.



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:1)


**וַיִּפָּקְדוּ.** לְשׁוֹן פְּקִידוּת:


**were appointed** Heb. וַיִפָקְדוּ, a word meaning appointment.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:2)


**הַנְּשָׁכוֹת.** כְּמוֹ הַלְּשָׁכוֹת שֶׁל בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**the chambers** Heb. הַנְשָׁכוֹת, like הַלְשָּׁכוֹת, the chambers of the Temple.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:44:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:3)


**לִכְנוֹס בָּהֶם.** וּבְאוֹתָן הַלְּשָׁכוֹת הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים כָּל מַתְּנוֹתֵיהֶם שֶׁל כֹּהֲנִים וּלְוִיִּם, וּמִשָּׁם הָיוּ חוֹלְקִין בֵּינֵיהֶם:


**to gather in them** And in these chambers they would place all the gifts of the priests and the Levites, and from there they would distribute [them] among them.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:44:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:4)


**מְנָאוֹת.** כְּמוֹ מָנוֹת (נחמיה ח:י):


**portions** Heb. מְנָאוֹת like מָנוֹת.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:44:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:5)


**לַכֹּהֲנִים לַלְוִיִּם.** כֹּהֲנִים הָיוּ נוֹטְלִים הָרָאוּי לָהֶם וְהַלְוִיִּם הָרָאוּי לָהֶם:


**for the priests and for the Levites** The priests were taking what was due them and the Levites what was due them.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:44:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:44:6)


**הָעוֹמְדִים.** וּמְשַׁמְּשִׁים שָׁם לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּתוֹדוֹת וְהוֹדָאוֹת:


**who stood** and ministered there before the Holy One, blessed be He, with thanksgiving offerings and thanksgivings.



### Verse 47

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:47:1)


**מְנָיוֹת.** כְּמוֹ מָנוֹת (נחמיה ח:י):


**the portions** Heb. מְנָיוֹת, like מָנוֹת.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 12:47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/12:47:2)


**וּמַקְדִּישִׁים לַלְוִיִּם.** הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים בְּמַעֲשֵׂר **לַלְוִיִּם**, וְהֵם הָיוּ נוֹטְלִים מַעֲשֵׂר מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר וְנוֹתְנִים לַכֹּהֲנִים:


**and they consecrated [them] for the Levites** And they would give the tithe to the Levites, who would take the tithe from the tithe and give it to the priests.



## Chapter 13


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:3:1)


**כָּל עֵרֶב.** כָּל תַּעֲרוֹבוֹת הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, כְּמוֹ ״וְגַם עֵרֶב רַב עָלָה אִתָּם״ (שמות יב:לח):


**all mixtures** Every mixture of heathens, like (Exodus 12:38): “A great mixed multitude (עֵרֶב רַב) also went up with them.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:4:1)


**וְלִפְנֵי מִזֶּה.** וּמִקֹּדֶם לָכֵן:


**Now prior to this** Heb. וְלִפְנֵי מִזֶה, before this.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:4:2)


**קָרוֹב לְטוֹבִיָּה.** אוֹתוֹ אֶלְיָשִׁיב הַכֹּהֵן הָיָה קָרוֹב לְטוֹבִיָּה חֲבֵרוֹ שֶׁל סַנְבַלַּט, וְנָתַן שָׁם הַכֹּהֵן כֵּלָיו שֶׁל טוֹבִיָּה:


**was a kinsman of Tobiah** Eliashib the priest was a kinsman of Tobiah the companion of Sanballat, and the priest placed Tobiah’s vessels there.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:5:1)


**וַיַּעַשׂ.** אֶלְיָשִׁיב:


**And he made** Eliashib [made].


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:5:2)


**לְפָנִים.** שָׁם הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים קֹדֶם שֶׁבָּאתִי לִירוּשָׁלָיִם:


**in previous times** Before I came to Jerusalem, they would put [them] there.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:5:3)


**מִצְוַת הַלְוִיִּם.** מַתָּנוֹת הָרְאוּיוֹת לַלְוִיִּם:


**which were ordained for the Levites** the gifts due the Levites.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:6:1)


**וּבְכָל זֶה.** וּבְכָל הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הַזֶּה **בָּאתִי אֶל הַמֶּלֶךְ** וְחָזַרְתִּי לְבָבֶל:


**Now with all this** during this entire episode. **I came to the king** when I returned to Babylon.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:6:2)


**נִשְׁאַלְתִּי.** שֶׁיִּתֵּן לִי רְשׁוּת לַעֲלוֹת לִירוּשָׁלַיִם וְלַחֲזוֹר:


**I requested of the king** to give me permission to go up and to return to Jerusalem.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:9:1)


**וָאֹמְרָה.** צִוִּיתִי לְטַהֵר, **וַיְטַהֲרוּ הַלְּשָׁכוֹת וְגוֹמֵר**:


**And I commanded** I commanded to purify it, and they purified the chambers, etc.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:10:1)


**מְנָיוֹת.** מָנוֹת (נחמיה ח:י):


**portions** Heb. מְנָיוֹת, [like] מָנוֹת.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:10:2)


**לֹא נִתָּנָה.** שֶׁלֹּא נָתְנוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל שָׁם הַמַּתָּנוֹת, וְעַל כֵּן בָּרְחוּ וְהָלְכוּ לָהֶם אָנָה וָאָנָה לִשְׁאֹל מַתְּנוֹתֵיהֶם, וְלֹא הָיוּ עוֹסְקִים כָּל כָּךְ בַּעֲבוֹדָתָם:


**had not been given** that the Israelites had not placed the gifts there, and because of that they fled and wandered here and there to ask for their gifts, and were not occupied as much with their service.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:11:1)


**וָאֶקְבְּצֵם.** קִבַּצְתִּי אֶת הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם לְהַעֲמִידָם עַל מִשְׁמַרְתָּם בַּעֲבוֹדָתָם, וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל הָיוּ מְבִיאִים לָהֶם שָׁם מַתְּנוֹתָם:


**And I gathered them** I gathered the priests and the Levites to station them on their watches in their service, and all of Israel brought them their gifts there.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:13:1)


**כִּי נֶאֱמָנִים.** הָיוּ לַחֲלֹק הַמַּתָּנוֹת בֵּינֵיהֶם:


**for they were deemed trustworthy** to distribute the gifts among them [the Levites].



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:15:1)


**הָעֲרֵמוֹת.** אֲלֻמּוֹת שֶׁל תְּבוּאָה:


**the stacks** sheaves of grain.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:15:2)


**וָאָעִיד.** הֵעִידוֹתִי בָּהֶם שֶׁלֹּא לַעֲשׂוֹת:


**and I warned** I warned them not to do it.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:15:3)


**בְּיוֹם מִכְרָם צָיִד.** בְּיוֹם שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹכְרִים מָזוֹן וְצַיִד, צַיִד כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּתֵּן לָהֶם צֵידָה לַדֶּרֶךְ״ (בראשית מה:כא):


**on the day they sold provisions** On the day they were selling food and provisions. צָיִד, provisions, as (Gen. 45:21): “...and he gave them provision (צֵדָה) for the way.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:16:1)


**וְהַצּוֹרִים.** סוֹחֲרִים שֶׁל צוֹר מְבִיאִים דָּגִים לִמְכּוֹר:


**and the Tyrian** Merchants of Tyre would bring fish to sell.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:19:1)


**וַיְהִי כַּאֲשֶׁר צָלְלוּ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר נָטוּ הַצְּלָלִים שֶׁל עֶרֶב **שַׁבָּת** בַּשְּׁעָרִים (ירמיהו ו:ד), צִוִּיתִי לִסְגֹּר דַּלְתוֹת הָעִיר, שֶׁלֹּא יִפָּתְחוּ עַד אַחַר שַׁבָּת, שֶׁלֹּא יָבוֹאוּ הָרוֹכְלִים בָּעִיר לִמְכֹּר סְחוֹרָתָן, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְחַלְּלוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת עַל יְדֵיהֶם:


**Now it came to pass when the gates of Jerusalem cast shadows** When the shadows of the Sabbath eve fell on the gates, I ordered the doors of the city to be closed and not re-opened until after the Sabbath so that the traffickers would not enter the city to sell their merchandise and cause Israel to profane the Sabbath.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:20:1)


**וַיָּלִינוּ.** וְהָיוּ לָנִים הָרוֹכְלִים וְהַסּוֹחֲרִים בְּיוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת מִחוּץ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם פַּעַם אַחַת וּשְׁתֵּי פְעָמִים, כְּדֵי שֶׁיָּבוֹאוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לִפְתֹּחַ הַשְּׁעָרִים וְלִקְנוֹת מֵהֶם בַּשַּׁבָּת:


**So...lodged** The traffickers and the merchants lodged outside Jerusalem on the Sabbath once and twice so that the Children of Israel would open the gates and buy from them on the Sabbath.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:21:1)


**וָאֹמְרָה אֲלֵיהֶם.** הִתְרֵיתִי בְּאוֹתָם הָרוֹכְלִים:


**and I said to them** I warned those traffickers.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:21:2)


**אִם תִּשְׁנוּ.** אִם תּוֹסִיפוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת פַּעַם שְׁנִיָּה:


**If you repeat this** if you continue to do this a second time.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:21:3)


**מִן הָעֵת.** מִן אוֹתוֹ יוֹם וָהָלְאָה **לֹא בָאוּ בְּיוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת**:


**Since that time** From that day on, they did not come on the Sabbath day.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:23:1)


**הוֹשִׁיבוּ נָשִׁים.** אֲשֶׁר נָשְׂאוּ לָהֶם נָשִׁים שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**who had married** who had married heathen wives.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:24:1)


**חֲצִי מְדַבֵּר.** הַרְבֵּה מִבְּנֵיהֶם הָיוּ מְדַבְּרִים לְשׁוֹן **אַשְׁדּוֹדִית** כְּאִמּוֹתָם:


**And half...were speaking** Many of their children spoke the Ashdodite language like their mothers.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:25:1)


**וָאַכֶּה.** נָתַתִּי לָהֶם מַלְקִיּוֹת לְהַלְקוֹתָם כְּדֵי לְהוֹכִיחָם וּלְהַזְכִּירָם:


**and I struck** I gave them lashes in order to chastise them and remind them.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:26:1)


**גַּם אוֹתוֹ הֶחֱטִיאוּ.** כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁתִּהְיוּ חוֹטְאִים עַל יְדֵי נְשֵׁיכֶם שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסָמוּךְ **וְלָכֶם הֲנִשְׁמַע וְגוֹ׳** בִּתְמִיהָּה. כְּלוֹמַר: שְׁלֹמֹה מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל נִמְצָא חָטָא עַל יְדֵי נָשִׁים שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, וְאַתֶּם נְשָׂאתֶם נָשִׁים שֶׁל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת לִהְיוֹת חוֹטְאִין:


**caused him even to sin** If he sinned, how much more so is it certain that you will sin through your heathen wives! As is written in the next verse, “Shall we hearken, etc.?” i.e., Solomon was found sinning through his heathen wives, and you have married foreign wives in order to sin?



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:28:1)


**וּמִבְּנֵי יוֹיָדָע... חָתָן לְסַנְבַּלַּט.** אֶחָד **מִבְּנֵי יוֹיָדָע חָתָן לְסַנְבַּלַּט** צוֹרֵר הַיְּהוּדִים, וְהִבְרַחְתִּיהוּ **מֵעָלָי**, לְמַעַן לֹא יִהְיֶה רָגִיל בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם בִּשְׁבִיל חֲתָנוֹ, לִרְאוֹת אֶת הָעִיר וְאֶת מוֹצָאֶיהָ וְאֶת מוֹבָאֶיהָ:


**And [one] of the sons of Joiada...was the son in law of Sanballat** One of the sons of Joiada was the son-in-law of Sanballat, the adversary of the Jews; thus, I drove him away from me so that he should not frequent Jerusalem because of his son-in-law, to see the city and the ways to enter and leave it.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:29:1)


**זָכְרָה לָהֶם.** הַטּוֹבָה אֲשֶׁר עָשׂוּ:


**Remember them** the good that they did.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:31:1)


**בְּעִתִּים.** לְעִתִּים הָיוּ כּוֹרְתִים עֵצִים לַמַּעֲרָכָה:


**at appointed times** They would cut wood for the altar pyre at appointed times.


###### Rashi on Nehemiah 13:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Nehemiah/r/13:31:2)


**וְלַבִּכּוּרִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְבִיאִים בִּכּוּרִים בְּפֶרֶק אֶחָד, לְכָל דָּבָר וְדָבָר קָבַעְתִּי זְמַנּוֹ:


**and concerning the first fruits** that they should bring the first fruits at a certain time; for each species I appointed its time.
